## p.1 <span id="page-1"></span>

<span id="S001"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001

**Original:** Improving Function Space Flow Matching with Kernel Optimal Transport

**中文:** 利用核最优传输改进函数空间流匹配

<span id="S002"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S002

**Original:** Fred Xu, Thomas Markovich, Barbora Barancikova, and Yizhou Sun. Fred Xu: University of California, Los Angeles; Thomas Markovich: Block, Inc.; Barbora Barancikova: Imperial College London; Yizhou Sun: University of California, Los Angeles. Emails: fredxu@cs.ucla.edu; tmarkovich@squareup.com; b.barancikova23@imperial.ac.uk; yzsun@cs.ucla.edu.

**中文:** 作者：Fred Xu、Thomas Markovich、Barbora Barancikova 和 Yizhou Sun。Fred Xu：加利福尼亚大学洛杉矶分校；Thomas Markovich：Block, Inc.；Barbora Barancikova：伦敦帝国理工学院；Yizhou Sun：加利福尼亚大学洛杉矶分校。电子邮箱依次为：fredxu@cs.ucla.edu、tmarkovich@squareup.com、b.barancikova23@imperial.ac.uk、yzsun@cs.ucla.edu。

<span id="S003"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S003

**Original:** Abstract. Generative models for function-valued data, such as time series and solutions of partial differential equations, must learn distributions over infinite-dimensional spaces. Functional Flow Matching (FFM) extends Flow Matching to this setting, learning a velocity field whose flow transports a Gaussian prior to the data distribution, but it inherits the independent endpoint pairing of standard Flow Matching: in each batch, prior and data samples are matched arbitrarily, so the conditional bridge must traverse both the shared global structure of the dataset and instance-specific residuals. In function space this limitation is harder to fix than in finite dimensions, since optimal transport (OT) on function spaces is delicate to formulate and a flat Euclidean surrogate ignores the geometry that distinguishes function-valued data. We propose kernel Functional Flow Matching (kFFM), which replaces the independent pairing by entropic OT under a kernel-induced cost, the coupling underlying the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence (HSD), while leaving the FFM neural-operator architecture unchanged. Theoretically, we prove that the kernel cost and the HSD objective are uniformly bounded and well-posed on Banach ambient spaces, derive an explicit error decomposition against quadratic-cost OT on compact metric spaces that isolates an irreducible kernel-cost mismatch term, and prove a discretization-invariance bound whose rate is governed by Sobolev regularity. Empirically, kFFM improves distributional matching over FFM, diffusion, adversarial, and finite-dimensional OT baselines on time-series and PDE benchmarks, with statistically significant paired-seed gains over FFM and improvements that persist under non-kernel and physics-based diagnostics, including a turbulent Navier–Stokes benchmark. Bounded kernel costs already outperform raw L2 Sinkhorn, and function-space-aware kernels (signature, Sobolev RBF) give further gains on rough or path-valued data.

**中文:** 摘要：对于时间序列和偏微分方程解等函数值数据，生成模型必须学习无限维空间上的分布。函数空间流匹配（Functional Flow Matching，FFM）将流匹配扩展到这一设置，通过学习速度场，使其生成的流将高斯先验输运至数据分布；但它继承了标准流匹配中端点独立配对的做法：在每个批次中，先验样本与数据样本被任意匹配，因此条件桥必须同时穿越数据集共享的全局结构和样本特有的残差。在函数空间中，这一限制比有限维情形更难解决，因为函数空间上的最优传输（OT）难以构造，而平坦的欧几里得替代度量忽略了区分函数值数据的几何结构。本文提出核函数空间流匹配（kernel Functional Flow Matching，kFFM），以核诱导代价下的熵正则 OT 替代独立配对；该耦合正是 Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度（HSD）所对应的耦合，同时保持 FFM 的神经算子架构不变。理论方面，本文证明核代价和 HSD 目标在 Banach 环境空间上具有一致有界性和良定性；推导了在紧度量空间上相对于二次代价 OT 的显式误差分解，并分离出不可约的核代价失配项；还证明了离散化不变性界，其收敛速率由 Sobolev 正则性决定。实验方面，在时间序列和偏微分方程基准上，kFFM 的分布匹配性能优于 FFM、扩散、对抗式和有限维 OT 基线；相较 FFM，其配对随机种子实验增益具有统计显著性，且在包括湍流 Navier–Stokes 基准在内的非核度量和物理诊断下仍有改善。有界核代价已优于原始 L2 Sinkhorn，而函数空间感知核（signature 核、Sobolev RBF）在粗糙数据或路径值数据上还能进一步提升性能。

<span id="S005"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S005

**Original:** 1 Introduction

**中文:** 1 引言


<span id="S006"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S006

**Original:** Flow matching [35] is a powerful framework for generative modeling that learns the velocity field of a probability path between a reference and a data distribution. Time series and PDE snapshots are naturally represented as functions, motivating Kerrigan et al. [25] to extend this framework to function space through Functional Flow Matching (FFM), which defines a path of Gaussian measures in a Hilbert space and parameterizes the velocity field with a neural operator. FFM enjoys resolution-invariance and competitive performance on sequence and PDE datasets, but inherits the independent endpoint coupling of base FM. Under this coupling, each Gaussian prior sample is paired with an arbitrary data function in a minibatch, and the conditional bridge must traverse both

**中文:** 流匹配[35]是一种强大的生成建模框架，它学习连接参考分布与数据分布的概率路径之速度场。时间序列和偏微分方程（PDE）快照天然可表示为函数，这促使 Kerrigan 等人[25]将该框架扩展到函数空间，提出函数空间流匹配（FFM）。FFM 在 Hilbert 空间中定义高斯测度路径，并用神经算子参数化速度场。FFM 具有分辨率不变性，在序列和 PDE 数据集上表现具有竞争力，但仍继承了基础流匹配（FM）的端点独立耦合方式。在这种耦合下，每个高斯先验样本都与小批次中任意一个数据函数配对，因此条件桥必须同时穿越……


## p.2 <span id="page-2"></span>

<span id="S007"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S007

**Original:** the shared global structure of the dataset and instance-specific residuals; the velocity field is then forced to discover the global structure and match each particular instance simultaneously. A natural fix is geometry-aware coupling: pairing each prior sample with a data sample that is already close in a meaningful geometry, so the bridge primarily transports the residual. This idea has improved finite-dimensional flow matching [48], but transferring it to function space is non-trivial: direct optimal transport (OT) on function spaces is delicate to formulate and compute [8, 9, 22, 37], and a flat L2 surrogate ignores the function-space geometry (Sobolev structure, path roughness) that distinguishes one function-valued sample from another.

**中文:** 数据集共享的全局结构和每个样本特有的残差；因此，速度场不得不同时发现全局结构并匹配每个具体样本。一种自然的改进是采用几何感知耦合：按照有意义的几何关系，将每个先验样本与一个已经相近的数据样本配对，使桥主要负责输运残差。这一思路已改进有限维流匹配[48]，但迁移到函数空间并非易事：函数空间上的直接最优传输（OT）在构造和计算上都很棘手[8,9,22,37]，而平坦的 L2 替代度量忽略了区分不同函数值样本的函数空间几何结构（Sobolev 结构、路径粗糙度）。

<span id="S008"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S008

**Original:** We resolve this through a kernel-induced Hilbert-space surrogate. Working in a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space [46], the entropic coupling underlying the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence [31] provides a tractable pairing rule whose kernel encodes prior knowledge about function-space geometry: Sobolev structure for PDE snapshots via the RBF kernel on H^k, and p-variation roughness for time series via the signature kernel [5, 6]. Our theory certifies this surrogate coupling (well-posedness with uniform bounds, an error decomposition with an irreducible kernel-mismatch term, and discretization invariance); it makes no straightness or sampling-efficiency claim in function space (Section 4, Appendix C.14), and the sample-quality gains are an empirical claim that we test with paired-seed statistics, non-kernel metrics, physics diagnostics, and a turbulent Navier–Stokes stress test. The kernel-induced surrogate also outperforms raw L2 Sinkhorn: relative to a flat-L2 minibatch-OT baseline (CFM-OT(L2)) with the same Sinkhorn solver and neural-operator backbone, kFFM lowers the distributional error by 75% on Gene Expression and 66% on Economy. Function-space-aware kernels (Sobolev RBF, signature) add further gains on rough or path-valued data. Code for every experiment is publicly available. Our main contributions are the following: (1) We propose kernel Functional Flow Matching (kFFM), to our knowledge the first method to perform OT endpoint coupling in function-space flow matching: a kernel-induced cost couples prior and data samples before flow matching, while the FFM neural-operator architecture is unchanged. (2) We establish three theoretical results for the function-space setting: uniform boundedness and well-posedness of the kernel cost and HSD on Banach ambient spaces; an explicit error decomposition against quadratic-cost OT on compact metric spaces (kernel mismatch, entropic bias, covering); and a discretization-invariance theorem with an N^{-2(α−k)/d} rate that ties the function-space objective to the truncated-grid representation the model actually computes. (3) We show empirically that kFFM improves population-level distributional quality on time series and PDE snapshots over functional generative baselines and a finite-dimensional OT coupling baseline, with paired-seed significance, gains that survive non-kernel and physics-based metrics including a turbulent Navier–Stokes benchmark, and a training overhead of only 5–7% at the largest resolution we train. The coupling geometry matters differently across data classes: signature kernels for paths, RBF-type costs for fields.

**中文:** 我们通过核诱导的 Hilbert 空间替代方案解决这一问题。在再生核 Hilbert 空间（RKHS）[46]中，Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度（HSD）[31]所对应的熵正则耦合提供了可计算的配对规则，其核编码关于函数空间几何的先验知识：对 PDE 快照，使用定义在 H^k 上的 RBF 核表达 Sobolev 结构；对时间序列，使用 signature 核[5,6]表达 p-变差粗糙度。本文理论分析证明该替代耦合具有良定性和一致有界性，给出包含不可约核失配项的误差分解，并证明离散化不变性；本文不声称函数空间中的输运路径更直或采样效率更高（第 4 节、附录 C.14）。样本质量提升属于实验结论，本文通过配对随机种子统计、非核度量、物理诊断和湍流 Navier–Stokes 压力测试进行检验。该核诱导替代方案也优于原始 L2 Sinkhorn：与使用相同 Sinkhorn 求解器和神经算子骨干的平坦 L2 小批次 OT 基线（CFM-OT(L2)）相比，kFFM 在 Gene Expression 和 Economy 数据集上的分布误差分别降低 75% 和 66%。函数空间感知核（Sobolev RBF、signature）在粗糙数据或路径值数据上还能进一步提升性能。所有实验代码均已公开。本文主要贡献如下：（1）提出核函数空间流匹配（kFFM）；据作者所知，这是首个在函数空间流匹配中执行 OT 端点耦合的方法：通过核诱导代价在流匹配前耦合先验样本与数据样本，同时保持 FFM 神经算子架构不变。（2）针对函数空间建立三项理论结果：核代价和 HSD 在 Banach 环境空间上的一致有界性与良定性；在紧度量空间上相对于二次代价 OT 的显式误差分解（核失配、熵正则偏差、覆盖误差）；以及离散化不变性定理，其速率为 N^{-2(α−k)/d}，将函数空间目标与模型实际计算的截断网格表示联系起来。（3）实验表明，kFFM 在时间序列和 PDE 快照上的总体分布质量优于函数生成基线和有限维 OT 耦合基线；配对随机种子结果具有显著性，且增益在非核度量和物理指标（包括湍流 Navier–Stokes 基准）下仍然成立，在所训练的最高分辨率下训练开销仅增加 5–7%。不同数据类别适用的耦合几何不同：路径数据适合 signature 核，场数据适合 RBF 类代价。

<span id="S009"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S009

**Original:** 2 Background

**中文:** 2 背景知识

<span id="S010"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S010

**Original:** For the FFM construction we follow Kerrigan et al. [25] and work in a separable Hilbert space F of functions f: Ω→R, with Ω⊂R^d compact, equipped with its Borel σ-algebra B(F); ν∈P(F) denotes the data measure. Section 2.1 works directly with F, while later subsections and Section 3 use X for the metric sample space on which the OT coupling is defined. Additional background is deferred to Appendix A.

**中文:** FFM 的构造遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]，并在由函数 f:Ω→R 构成的可分 Hilbert 空间 F 中进行，其中 Ω⊂R^d 为紧集，F 配备其 Borel σ-代数 B(F)；ν∈P(F) 表示数据测度。第 2.1 节直接使用 F，而后续小节和第 3 节使用 X 表示定义 OT 耦合所依据的度量样本空间。其他背景知识见附录 A。

<span id="S011"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S011

**Original:** 2.1 Flow Matching in Function Space

**中文:** 2.1 函数空间中的流匹配

<span id="S012"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S012

**Original:** Flow Matching [35] learns the velocity field whose flow transports a prior distribution to the data distribution along a given probability path. In the Gaussian-measure formulation of FFM, a vector field v:[0,1]×F→F induces the flow ∂_t ψ_t(g)=v_t(ψ_t(g)), ψ_0(g)=g, and the path μ_t=[ψ_t]_# μ_0 from μ_0∈P(F). FFM constructs this path by averaging endpoint-conditioned Gaussian bridges over f∼ν: if μ_0^f=μ_0 and μ_1^f is concentrated around f, then μ_t(A)=∫ μ_t^f(A)dν(f) satisfies μ_1=ν. For Gaussian bridges with μ_0=N(0,C_0) and μ_t^f=N(m_t^f,(σ_t^f)^2 C_0), the conditional velocity formula follows on the next page.

**中文:** 流匹配[35]学习一个速度场，使该速度场生成的流沿给定概率路径将先验分布输运至数据分布。在 FFM 的高斯测度表述中，向量场 v:[0,1]×F→F 诱导流 ∂_tψ_t(g)=v_t(ψ_t(g))、ψ_0(g)=g，并由 μ_0∈P(F) 生成路径 μ_t=[ψ_t]_#μ_0。FFM 对 f∼ν 的端点条件高斯桥取平均来构造该路径：若 μ_0^f=μ_0，且 μ_1^f 集中于 f 附近，则 μ_t(A)=∫μ_t^f(A)dν(f)，并满足 μ_1=ν。对于 μ_0=N(0,C_0)、μ_t^f=N(m_t^f,(σ_t^f)^2C_0) 的高斯桥，其条件速度公式见下一页。

<span id="S013"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S013

**Original:** Code for every experiment: https://github.com/heraclixus/KFFM

**中文:** 全部实验代码：https://github.com/heraclixus/KFFM



## p.3 <span id="page-3"></span>

<span id="S014"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S014

**Original:** For the Gaussian bridges with μ_0=N(0,C_0) and μ_t^f=N(m_t^f,(σ_t^f)^2 C_0), the conditional velocity is v_t^f(g)=((σ_t^f)′/σ_t^f)(g−m_t^f)+(d m_t^f/dt), g∼μ_t^f. The standard FFM parameterization m_t^f=tf and σ_t^f=1−(1−σ_min)t corresponds to Gaussian OT between the bridge marginals, while the endpoint pairing itself remains independent [48]. A neural operator v_θ(g,t), here a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) [32], is trained with the Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) loss L_CFM(θ):=E_{t∼U[0,1], f∼ν, g∼μ_t^f}[||v_t^f(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F²], (1) which under suitable regularity conditions [25,35] has the same minimizers as the unconditional loss L_FM(θ):=E_{t∼U[0,1],g∼μ_t}[||v_t(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F²]; the absolute-continuity assumption behind this equivalence is given in Appendix B.2. Section 3 keeps the same probability path but replaces the independent endpoint pairing by an OT-coupled pairing.

**中文:** 对于 μ_0=N(0,C_0)、μ_t^f=N(m_t^f,(σ_t^f)^2C_0) 的高斯桥，条件速度为 v_t^f(g)=((σ_t^f)′/σ_t^f)(g−m_t^f)+(dm_t^f/dt)，其中 g∼μ_t^f。标准 FFM 参数化 m_t^f=tf、σ_t^f=1−(1−σ_min)t 对应于桥边缘分布之间的高斯 OT，但端点配对本身仍然是独立的[48]。本文使用神经算子 v_θ(g,t)——具体为傅里叶神经算子（FNO）[32]——通过条件流匹配（CFM）损失进行训练：L_CFM(θ):=E_{t∼U[0,1],f∼ν,g∼μ_t^f}[||v_t^f(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F²]。（1）在适当正则性条件下[25,35]，该损失与无条件损失 L_FM(θ):=E_{t∼U[0,1],g∼μ_t}[||v_t(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F²] 具有相同的极小化解；这一等价关系所需的绝对连续性假设见附录 B.2。第 3 节保持相同概率路径，但将端点独立配对替换为 OT 耦合配对。

<span id="S015"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S015

**Original:** 2.2 Optimal Transport and Kernel-based Optimal Transport. For (X,d) a Polish space, μ,ν∈P(X), and a cost c, the static OT problem is W(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫c dπ [49], and its entropic regularization is W_ε(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}[∫c(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)], (2) which underlies Sinkhorn methods; the debiased Sinkhorn divergence subtracts the corresponding self-costs [16,17,47]. OT-based couplings improve finite-dimensional flow matching by computing a static coupling between reference and data samples before interpolation [28,48]. Adapting this idea to function-valued data is non-trivial: no Lebesgue-like reference measure exists on function space [11,37], so densities are ill-posed, and Gaussian-measure and Cameron–Martin assumptions [8] are limiting when data are non-Gaussian (PDE states, rough paths). This motivates embedding measures into a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS) [46], where coupling is computed under a kernel-induced cost.

**中文:** 2.2 最优传输与基于核的最优传输。设 (X,d) 为 Polish 空间，μ,ν∈P(X)，且 c 为代价函数，则静态 OT 问题为 W(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫c dπ[49]；其熵正则化形式为 W_ε(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}[∫c(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)]。（2）该形式构成 Sinkhorn 方法的基础；去偏 Sinkhorn 散度则减去相应的自代价[16,17,47]。基于 OT 的耦合通过在插值前计算参考样本与数据样本之间的静态耦合，改进了有限维流匹配[28,48]。将这一思路用于函数值数据并不容易：函数空间不存在类似 Lebesgue 测度的参考测度[11,37]，因而密度的定义不适定；当数据为非高斯形式（如 PDE 状态、粗糙路径）时，高斯测度和 Cameron–Martin 假设也具有局限性[8]。因此，可将测度嵌入再生核 Hilbert 空间（RKHS）[46]，并在核诱导代价下计算耦合。

<span id="S016"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S016

**Original:** Definition 1 (Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence [31]). Let (X,d) be a metric space, μ,ν∈P(X), and κ:X×X→R a measurable symmetric positive-definite kernel with RKHS H_κ and canonical feature map φ:X→H_κ. The entropic OT functional on the embedded space is OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν):=inf_{π_φ∈Π(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)}[∫||u−v||²_{H_κ}dπ_φ(u,v)+ε KL(π_φ||φ_#μ⊗φ_#ν)], (3) and the Hilbert Sinkhorn divergence is the debiased quantity S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν):=OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)−(1/2)OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#μ)−(1/2)OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#ν,φ_#ν).

**中文:** 定义 1（Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度[31]）。设 (X,d) 为度量空间，μ,ν∈P(X)，κ:X×X→R 为可测、对称、正定核，其 RKHS 为 H_κ，规范特征映射为 φ:X→H_κ。嵌入空间上的熵正则 OT 泛函定义为 OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν):=inf_{π_φ∈Π(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)}[∫||u−v||²_{H_κ}dπ_φ(u,v)+ε KL(π_φ||φ_#μ⊗φ_#ν)]。（3）Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度是去偏后的量：S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν):=OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)−(1/2)OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#μ)−(1/2)OT^H_{ε,κ}(φ_#ν,φ_#ν)。

<span id="S017"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S017

**Original:** By the kernel trick, the functional in (3) can be written directly on X with the RKHS-induced cost c_κ(x,y):=||φ(x)−φ(y)||²_{H_κ}=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y) [31, restated as Proposition 3 in Appendix B]: OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}[∫_{X×X}c_κ(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)], (4) with minimizers corresponding under pushforward, and S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν):=OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν)=S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν). Previous work takes X⊂R^d, where common kernels apply directly, the divergence enjoys statistical consistency with finite-sample bounds (restated in Appendix B), and the discrete entropic OT problem is convex [16]. Section 3 generalizes X to function-valued sample spaces.

**中文:** 利用核技巧，式（3）的泛函可直接在 X 上改写，采用 RKHS 诱导代价 c_κ(x,y):=||φ(x)−φ(y)||²_{H_κ}=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y)[31；附录 B 命题 3 重述]：OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}[∫_{X×X}c_κ(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)]。（4）两种表述下的极小化耦合在推前映射下相对应，且 S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν):=OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν)=S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)。以往工作通常取 X⊂R^d，此时常用核可直接应用，散度具有带有限样本界的统计一致性（附录 B 重述），离散熵正则 OT 问题也是凸问题[16]。第 3 节将 X 推广到函数值样本空间。

<span id="S018"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S018

**Original:** 2.3 Path Signature and Signature Kernel

**中文:** 2.3 路径 signature 与 signature 核

<span id="S019"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S019

**Original:** Let C_p(R^d)=C_p([0,T],R^d) denote continuous paths from [0,T] to R^d with finite p-variation, p∈[1,2). The signature of x∈C_p(R^d) is the sequence of iterated Young integrals S(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),S^(2)(x),...), where S^(m)(x)=∫_{t_1<...<t_m} dx_{t_1}⊗...⊗dx_{t_m}∈(R^d)^{⊗m}, an element of the free tensor algebra T((R^d))=∏_{n=0}^∞(R^d)^{⊗n}.

**中文:** 令 C_p(R^d)=C_p([0,T],R^d) 表示从 [0,T] 到 R^d 的有限 p-变差连续路径，其中 p∈[1,2)。对 x∈C_p(R^d)，其 signature 是迭代 Young 积分序列 S(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),S^(2)(x),...)；其中 S^(m)(x)=∫_{t_1<...<t_m}dx_{t_1}⊗...⊗dx_{t_m}∈(R^d)^{⊗m}，它属于自由张量代数 T((R^d))=∏_{n=0}^∞(R^d)^{⊗n)。



## p.4 <span id="page-4"></span>

<span id="S020"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S020

**Original:** Definition 2 (Signature Kernel [5]). The signature kernel κ_sig:C_p(R^d)×C_p(R^d)→R is κ_sig(x,y)=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩:=Σ_{m=0}^∞⟨S^(m)(x),S^(m)(y)⟩_{(R^d)^{⊗m}}, where ⟨·,·⟩_{(R^d)^{⊗m}} is the Hilbert–Schmidt inner product. The signature kernel is symmetric and positive definite and hence induces a unique RKHS H_sig; on compact subsets, the time-augmented signature kernel is universal and characteristic [5]. We compute it with pySigLib [44].

**中文:** 定义 2（signature 核[5]）。signature 核 κ_sig:C_p(R^d)×C_p(R^d)→R 定义为 κ_sig(x,y)=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩:=Σ_{m=0}^∞⟨S^(m)(x),S^(m)(y)⟩_{(R^d)^{⊗m}}，其中 ⟨·,·⟩_{(R^d)^{⊗m}} 为 Hilbert–Schmidt 内积。signature 核对称且正定，因此诱导唯一的 RKHS H_sig；在紧子集上，加入时间增强的 signature 核是通用且特征性的[5]。本文使用 pySigLib[44]计算该核。

<span id="S021"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S021

**Original:** 3 Functional Flow Matching with Kernel Optimal Transport

**中文:** 3 基于核最优传输的函数空间流匹配

<span id="S022"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S022

**Original:** Throughout this section X is a general metric space, the sample space on which data and reference measures live and on which the kernel OT coupling acts.

**中文:** 本节中，X 表示一般度量空间，即数据测度和参考测度所在的样本空间，也是核 OT 耦合作用的空间。

<span id="S023"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S023

**Original:** 3.1 Coupling with Kernel Optimal Transport

**中文:** 3.1 使用核最优传输进行耦合

<span id="S024"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S024

**Original:** kFFM first couples prior and data samples through entropic OT under a kernel-induced cost, Equation (4). The kernel reflects the metric space where the data live. On the Sobolev space X=H^k(Ω) (functions with k weak derivatives in L2, the natural home of PDE solutions [12]), the RBF kernel on H^k rewards pairs close in a derivative-aware norm; on path space X⊂C_p([0,T],R^d), p∈[1,2) (smooth and rough time series [5]), the signature kernel rewards reparameterization-invariant path similarity. Well-posedness for each choice is established in Section 3.2 and validated in Section 4; with empirical measures, the minibatch problem is solved by the Sinkhorn algorithm [17].

**中文:** kFFM 首先使用式（4）中的核诱导代价，通过熵正则 OT 耦合先验样本和数据样本。核反映数据所在的度量空间。在 Sobolev 空间 X=H^k(Ω) 上（其中函数具有 k 阶 L2 弱导数，该空间是 PDE 解的自然归属空间[12]），定义在 H^k 上的 RBF 核会偏好在考虑导数的范数下相近的样本对；在路径空间 X⊂C_p([0,T],R^d)、p∈[1,2) 上（包括光滑和粗糙时间序列[5]），signature 核会偏好具有重参数化不变相似性的路径。第 3.2 节证明各类选择的良定性，第 4 节进行验证；对于经验测度，小批次问题通过 Sinkhorn 算法求解[17]。

<span id="S025"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S025

**Original:** Given a plan π between prior draws f_0∼μ_0 and data samples f_1∼μ_1, kFFM uses the same probability path as FFM, with the paired prior sample in the role of base noise: g_t^{f_0,f_1}=t f_1+σ_t f_0, σ_t=1−(1−σ_min)t, (f_0,f_1)∼π; v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g_t^{f_0,f_1})=∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0=f_1/σ_t−((1−σ_min)/σ_t)g_t^{f_0,f_1}. Thus g_0=f_0∼μ_0, g_1=f_1+σ_min f_0, and the conditional velocity is the FFM conditional field of Section 2.1 evaluated at the coupled pair; the FFM path tf+σ_t ξ is recovered exactly for independent coupling π=μ_0⊗μ_1.

**中文:** 给定先验样本 f_0∼μ_0 与数据样本 f_1∼μ_1 之间的耦合计划 π，kFFM 使用与 FFM 相同的概率路径，并将配对的先验样本作为基础噪声：g_t^{f_0,f_1}=t f_1+σ_t f_0，σ_t=1−(1−σ_min)t，(f_0,f_1)∼π；v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g_t^{f_0,f_1})=∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0=f_1/σ_t−((1−σ_min)/σ_t)g_t^{f_0,f_1}。因此，g_0=f_0∼μ_0，g_1=f_1+σ_min f_0；条件速度即第 2.1 节 FFM 条件速度场在耦合样本对处的取值。若使用独立耦合 π=μ_0⊗μ_1，则可精确恢复 FFM 路径 tf+σ_tξ。

<span id="S026"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S026

**Original:** What the coupling changes.

**中文:** 耦合改变了什么。

<span id="S027"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S027

**Original:** Up to the σ_min term, the regression target is the displacement u:=f_1−f_0, the residual between paired data and prior samples, which carries all endpoint information. Under independent coupling this residual is the difference of two unrelated draws, a large and high-variance regression target; under kernel-OT coupling each f_1 is paired with a geometrically close f_0, so the network regresses smaller, structured residuals. The neural operator (an FNO) is unchanged relative to FFM; only the minibatch coupling, and through it the law of the conditional paths, is modified. Algorithm 1 summarizes training; implementation defaults and good practices are in Appendix C.8. The reference distribution μ_0 is a Gaussian process (GP) or Gaussian random field [19], or white noise on the grid where the data are rougher than a smooth GP prior (the base measure is part of the selected configuration, Section 4.1); in practice both measures are replaced by empirical estimators, with the coupling computed from a kernel-induced cost matrix [17,31]: μ̂_n=(1/n)Σ_{i=1}^nδ_{x_i}, ν̂_n=(1/n)Σ_{j=1}^nδ_{y_j}, C^κ_{ij}=κ(x_i,x_i)+κ(y_j,y_j)−2κ(x_i,y_j). (5)

**中文:** 除 σ_min 项外，回归目标是位移 u:=f_1−f_0，即配对数据样本与先验样本之间承载全部端点信息的残差。在独立耦合下，该残差是两个互不相关样本之差，幅度大且方差高；在核 OT 耦合下，每个 f_1 与几何上接近的 f_0 配对，因此网络回归的是更小且结构化的残差。与 FFM 相比，神经算子（FNO）保持不变；改变的只有小批次耦合，以及由此产生的条件路径分布。算法 1 总结训练流程；实现默认设置和实践建议见附录 C.8。参考分布 μ_0 可取高斯过程（GP）或高斯随机场[19]；若数据比光滑 GP 先验更粗糙，也可在网格上使用白噪声（基础测度属于第 4.1 节所选配置的一部分）。实际中，两种测度均以经验估计量替代，并根据核诱导代价矩阵计算耦合[17,31]：μ̂_n=(1/n)Σ_{i=1}^nδ_{x_i}，ν̂_n=(1/n)Σ_{j=1}^nδ_{y_j}，C^κ_{ij}=κ(x_i,x_i)+κ(y_j,y_j)−2κ(x_i,y_j)。（5）

<span id="S028"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S028

**Original:** Where the kernel enters.

**中文:** 核在何处发挥作用。

<span id="S029"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S029

**Original:** The kernel enters at exactly one place: the cost c_κ used by minibatch Sinkhorn to pair prior and data samples. No sample or model input is projected into the RKHS; the FNO acts on the original discretized functions, paths are constructed in the original space, and generation happens there. The training coupling is the entropic plan induced by c_κ, computed per minibatch with log-domain Sinkhorn on a median-normalized cost and sampled from the plan (Appendix C.8); the debiasing self-terms of the HSD (Definition 1) do not depend on π and cannot change the argmin plan, so HSD is our analysis object (what Theorems 1–2 control), not the training objective. Finally, at fixed batch size b, minibatch OT induces the expected minibatch plan π̄_b=E[π̂_b] [13,14], a valid coupling of the true marginals for every b, so the CFM consistency argument applies and b only changes which conditional paths are used; as b→∞, π̄_b converges to the population entropic kernel-OT plan.

**中文:** 核只在一个位置发挥作用：小批次 Sinkhorn 用于配对先验样本与数据样本的代价 c_κ。没有任何样本或模型输入被投影到 RKHS；FNO 仍作用于原始离散函数，路径在原始空间中构造，生成也在该空间中进行。训练耦合是由 c_κ 诱导的熵正则计划：对每个小批次，基于中位数归一化的代价使用对数域 Sinkhorn 求解，并从该计划采样（附录 C.8）。HSD（定义 1）的去偏自项与 π 无关，因此不会改变极小化耦合计划；故 HSD 是本文的分析对象（定理 1–2 对其进行控制），而非训练目标。最后，在固定批次大小 b 下，小批次 OT 诱导的期望批次计划为 π̄_b=E[π̂_b][13,14]，对于任意 b 都是真实边缘分布之间的有效耦合。因此 CFM 一致性论证仍然适用，b 仅改变所使用的条件路径；当 b→∞ 时，π̄_b 收敛到总体熵正则核 OT 计划。



## p.5 <span id="page-5"></span>

<span id="S030"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S030

**Original:** Algorithm 1. Functional Flow Matching with Kernel Optimal Transport. Require: Prior distribution μ_0=N(0,C_0), data distribution μ_1, kernel function κ, entropy regularization ε>0, batch size b, initial network v_θ, σ_min>0. 1: Training loop (repeat until convergence): 2: Sample batches of size b: f_0∼μ_0, f_1∼μ_1. 3: Solve the entropic kernel OT problem in Equation (4) with κ, ε (cost matrix in Equation (5)) to get coupling π. 4: (f_0,f_1)∼π. 5: t∼U(0,1), σ_t←1−(1−σ_min)t. 6: g←tf_1+σ_tf_0 //the paired prior sample plays the role of the base noise. 7: v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g)←(f_1−(1−σ_min)g)/σ_t=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0. 8: L_CFM(θ)←||v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F². 9: θ←Update(θ,∇_θL_CFM(θ)). 10: Output: v_θ.

**中文:** 算法 1. 基于核最优传输的函数空间流匹配。
输入：先验分布 μ_0=N(0,C_0)、数据分布 μ_1、核函数 κ、熵正则系数 ε>0、批大小 b、初始网络 v_θ、σ_min>0。
1：重复执行训练循环直至收敛。
2：分别从 μ_0、μ_1 中抽取批大小为 b 的样本 f_0、f_1。
3：使用 κ、ε 求解式（4）的熵正则核 OT 问题（代价矩阵见式（5）），获得耦合 π。
4：从 π 中抽取样本对 (f_0,f_1)。
5：采样 t∼U(0,1)，并令 σ_t←1−(1−σ_min)t。
6：令 g←tf_1+σ_tf_0；配对的先验样本在此充当基础噪声。
7：令 v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g)←(f_1−(1−σ_min)g)/σ_t=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0。
8：令 L_CFM(θ)←||v_t^{f_0,f_1}(g)−v_θ(g,t)||_F²。
9：按 θ←Update(θ,∇_θL_CFM(θ)) 更新 θ。
10：输出 v_θ。

<span id="S031"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S031

**Original:** 3.2 Theoretical Results for Sobolev and Path Space

**中文:** 3.2 Sobolev 空间与路径空间的理论结果

<span id="S032"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S032

**Original:** The theory certifies the surrogate coupling; it does not by itself explain why sample quality improves, which Section 4 establishes empirically. It answers three questions (proofs in Appendix B): Is the kernel-OT objective well-defined and well-conditioned on the function spaces used by kFFM? How far is the RKHS-induced cost from quadratic-cost OT, and which parts of the gap are reducible? Does the continuum formulation survive the Fourier truncation used by the FNO and Sinkhorn solver?

**中文:** 本文理论结果用于认证该替代耦合；理论本身并不能解释样本质量为何提高，这一点由第 4 节的实验加以验证。理论部分回答三个问题（证明见附录 B）：kFFM 所用函数空间上的核 OT 目标是否定义良好且条件良好？RKHS 诱导代价与二次代价 OT 相差多远，其中哪些误差部分可以缩小？连续空间表述在经过 FNO 和 Sinkhorn 求解器所用的傅里叶截断后是否仍成立？

<span id="S033"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S033

**Original:** Theorem 1 (Well-posedness and uniform boundedness). Let (X,||·||_X) be a real Banach space and d_X(f,g):=||f−g||_X. Let μ,ν∈P(X), and let κ:X×X→R be measurable, symmetric, and positive definite with RKHS H_κ. Assume κ is bounded: B_κ:=sup_{f∈X}κ(f,f)<∞. Then 0≤c_κ(f,g)≤4B_κ for all f,g∈X, and for every ε>0 the Hilbert Sinkhorn divergence S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν) (Definition 1) is finite for all μ,ν∈P(X), with |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤8B_κ.

**中文:** 定理 1（良定性与一致有界性）。设 (X,||·||_X) 为实 Banach 空间，d_X(f,g):=||f−g||_X。设 μ,ν∈P(X)，且 κ:X×X→R 为可测、对称、正定核，其 RKHS 为 H_κ。再假设 κ 有界，即 B_κ:=sup_{f∈X}κ(f,f)<∞。则对任意 f,g∈X，有 0≤c_κ(f,g)≤4B_κ；并且对于任意 ε>0，Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度 S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)（定义 1）对所有 μ,ν∈P(X) 都有限，且满足 |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤8B_κ。

<span id="S034"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S034

**Original:** Finiteness per se is not the binding constraint: under a second-moment assumption on ν the L2 cost is already integrable, and for the Gaussian prior E||f_0||²=tr(C_0)<∞. Theorem 1 delivers more. (a) Uniform boundedness with no assumption on the measures, which conditions the entropic solver: the Gibbs kernel e^{−c_κ/ε} stays bounded away from 0 and 1 across batches, so Sinkhorn converges independently of outlier pairs, unlike unbounded L2 costs on heavy-tailed batches (Section 4.3 quantifies the resulting speed-up). (b) Generality: the statement holds on Banach spaces without Hilbert structure, which licenses the signature kernel on path space, our strongest configuration. (c) Geometry injection: the kernel is the interface through which domain structure enters the coupling. For Sobolev data in H^k(Ω) we use κ_RBF^(k)(x,y):=exp(−||x−y||²_{H^k}/(2σ²)), which is bounded and positive definite [7,54]; for paths we use the signature kernel (Definition 2), universal and characteristic on compact sets [5], bounded there by continuity, and used for two-sample tests on path data [6], applied to time-augmented paths (Appendix A, Definition 14) to avoid its tree-like equivalence.

**中文:** 有限性本身并非主要限制：若 ν 满足二阶矩条件，则 L2 代价已经可积；对于高斯先验，E||f_0||²=tr(C_0)<∞。定理 1 还给出了更强结论。（a）无需对测度作任何假设即可得到一致有界性，从而改善熵正则求解器的条件：Gibbs 核 e^{−c_κ/ε} 在各批次中远离 0 和 1，因此 Sinkhorn 收敛不受离群样本对影响；这不同于重尾批次中无界的 L2 代价（第 4.3 节量化了由此带来的加速）。（b）普适性：结论适用于不具备 Hilbert 结构的 Banach 空间，因此也支持在路径空间上使用 signature 核，这是本文表现最强的配置。（c）几何结构注入：核是将数据域结构引入耦合的接口。对于 H^k(Ω) 中的 Sobolev 数据，使用 κ_RBF^(k)(x,y):=exp(−||x−y||²_{H^k}/(2σ²))，该核有界且正定[7,54]；对于路径，使用定义 2 的 signature 核，该核在紧集上通用且具有特征性[5]，并由连续性而有界；它还用于路径数据的双样本检验[6]。本文将其应用于时间增强路径（附录 A，定义 14），以避免树状等价问题。

<span id="S035"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S035

**Original:** The entropic OT functional transports with the cost induced by the feature map rather than the original metric cost. On compact subsets of the ambient space (the H^α ball is compact in H^k for α>k; compact sets of paths for the signature kernel, with truncation discussed in Appendix B), the next theorem separates the resulting kernel-cost mismatch from entropic and covering effects.

**中文:** 熵正则 OT 泛函采用特征映射诱导的代价进行输运，而非原始度量代价。在环境空间的紧子集上（当 α>k 时，H^α 球在 H^k 中紧；signature 核对应路径的紧集，截断问题见附录 B），下一定理将由此产生的核代价失配与熵正则和覆盖效应区分开来。

<span id="S036"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S036

**Original:** Theorem 2 (Error decomposition). Let (X,d) be a compact metric space and μ,ν∈P(X). Define the target quadratic cost c(x,y)=d(x,y)^2. Let κ:X×X→R be a bounded positive-definite kernel with RKHS-induced cost c_κ(x,y):=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y), and let Δ:=sup_{x,y∈X}|c_κ(x,y)−c(x,y)|. Let OT_{κ,ε} denote the entropic OT functional in Equation (4) with cost c_κ, and let M:=diam(X)<∞. Then for every ε>0 and every δ∈(0,M]: |OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+2ε log N(X,δ;d)+8Mδ, (6) where N(X,δ;d) is the δ-covering number of X in metric d.

**中文:** 定理 2（误差分解）。设 (X,d) 为紧度量空间，μ,ν∈P(X)。定义目标二次代价 c(x,y)=d(x,y)^2。设 κ:X×X→R 为有界正定核，其 RKHS 诱导代价为 c_κ(x,y):=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y)，并令 Δ_κ:=sup_{x,y∈X}|c_κ(x,y)−c(x,y)|。令 OT_{κ,ε} 表示采用代价 c_κ 的式（4）熵正则 OT 泛函，并令 M:=diam(X)<∞。则对任意 ε>0 及 δ∈(0,M]，有 |OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+2ε log N(X,δ;d)+8Mδ。（6）其中 N(X,δ;d) 是度量 d 下 X 的 δ-覆盖数。



## p.6 <span id="page-6"></span>

<span id="S037"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S037

**Original:** Theorem 2 is an error decomposition, not an approximation guarantee: the entropic and covering terms vanish as ε,δ→0, but Δ_κ is irreducible and does not shrink with more samples or finer grids. For the RBF kernel, c_RBF=2(1−e^{−||f−g||²/2σ²}) is a rescaled L2 cost up to relative error O((D/σ)²), with D the batch diameter; with median-normalized costs Δ_κ is small at large bandwidth, and on smooth Navier–Stokes fields the induced plans agree across costs (total variation ≤0.02; Appendix C.12). For the signature kernel Δ_κ is large by design: the theorem then controls deviation from Wasserstein with the chosen cost, not proximity to L2 OT, and pair rankings differ measurably from L2 (rank correlation 0.33–0.37 on AEMET and gene expression), which is where the largest gains occur. In low-regularity regimes the bound degrades on two fronts: Δ_κ grows because a smooth kernel cost saturates on rough functions, and the covering number blows up as effective smoothness approaches the embedding threshold, making the entropic term large for any practical ε. The benchmarks of Section 4.1 sit in the smooth regime where the bound is informative; Section 4.2 stress-tests the turbulent regime empirically.

**中文:** 定理 2 给出的是误差分解，而非近似保证：当 ε、δ→0 时，熵正则项和覆盖项趋于零，但 Δ_κ 是不可约项，不会因增加样本或细化网格而缩小。对于 RBF 核，c_RBF=2(1−e^{−||f−g||²/(2σ²)}) 是经缩放的 L2 代价，相对误差为 O((D/σ)²)，其中 D 是批次直径；使用中位数归一化代价且带宽较大时，Δ_κ 较小；对于光滑 Navier–Stokes 场，不同代价诱导的计划也较一致（总变差≤0.02；附录 C.12）。对于 signature 核，Δ_κ 按设计会较大：此时该定理控制的是相对于所选代价 Wasserstein 距离的偏差，而非与 L2 OT 的接近程度；样本对的排序与 L2 有可测差异（AEMET 和基因表达数据上的秩相关为 0.33–0.37），而这些场景也正是增益最大的地方。在低正则性区域，该界在两个方面会变差：光滑核代价在粗糙函数上趋于饱和，使 Δ_κ 增大；有效光滑度接近嵌入阈值时，覆盖数急剧增大，使实际 ε 下的熵项变大。第 4.1 节的基准处于该界具有解释力的光滑区域；第 4.2 节则通过实验压力测试湍流区域。

<span id="S038"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S038

**Original:** Theorems 1 and 2 are stated at the continuum level, while the model operates on a finite-rank spectral representation: PDE snapshots are represented in a Fourier subspace of dimension proportional to N, which both the FNO and the Sinkhorn solver consume. Our final theorem quantifies the resulting gap at a rate governed by Sobolev regularity; the path-space analogue for signature truncation is discussed in Appendix B. Theorem 3 (Discretization invariance). Let Ω⊂R^d be a bounded periodic domain and let α>k≥0. Set X:=B_R^{H^α}:={f∈H^α(Ω):||f||_{H^α}<R}, viewed as a compact subset of H^k(Ω), and let μ,ν∈P(X). For Fourier basis {e_n}_{n∈Z^d}, let V_N:=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}} and let P_N:H^α(Ω)→V_N denote the orthogonal projection; hence dim V_N ≍N. Write μ_N=(P_N)_#μ and ν_N=(P_N)_#ν. Define the Sobolev-RBF kernel κ_RBF^(k)(f,g):=exp(−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²)) on H^k, and write KOT_ε^(k):=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε} for the corresponding entropic kernel-OT functional from Theorem 2. Then for every ε>0, |KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. Equivalently, in terms of grid spacing h=N^{−1/d}, the discretization error decays as O(h^{2(α−k)}).

**中文:** 定理 1 和定理 2 在连续空间层面表述，而模型使用有限秩谱表示：PDE 快照表示在维数与 N 成正比的傅里叶子空间中，FNO 和 Sinkhorn 求解器都使用这一表示。最后一个定理以 Sobolev 正则性决定的速率量化由此产生的误差；signature 截断在路径空间中的对应结果见附录 B。定理 3（离散化不变性）。设 Ω⊂R^d 为有界周期区域，且 α>k≥0。令 X:=B_R^{H^α}:={f∈H^α(Ω):||f||_{H^α}<R}，将其视为 H^k(Ω) 的紧子集，并设 μ,ν∈P(X)。对傅里叶基 {e_n}_{n∈Z^d}，令 V_N:=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}}，并令 P_N:H^α(Ω)→V_N 为正交投影，因此 dim V_N≍N。记 μ_N=(P_N)_#μ、ν_N=(P_N)_#ν。在 H^k 上定义 Sobolev-RBF 核 κ_RBF^(k)(f,g):=exp(−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²))，并以 KOT_ε^(k):=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε} 表示定理 2 中相应的熵正则核 OT 泛函。则对任意 ε>0，|KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。等价地，若网格间距 h=N^{−1/d}，离散化误差按 O(h^{2(α−k)}) 衰减。

<span id="S039"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S039

**Original:** Combining Theorems 2 and 3 bounds the gap between the computed finite-rank kernel-OT objective and continuum quadratic OT in the H^k metric by four interpretable terms: kernel-cost mismatch, entropic regularization, covering, and discretization (Corollary 1 in Appendix B.6). Only the discretization term vanishes with projection rank N, at rate N^{−2(α−k)/d} driven by regularity gap α−k, the formal counterpart of the empirical resolution-invariance in Section 4; the mismatch term depends only on kernel design (zero for the linear kernel, finite for RBF and controlled by data scale and bandwidth σ). The theorem relies on compact Sobolev embedding [12]; our smooth PDE benchmarks sit in that regime [4].

**中文:** 结合定理 2 和定理 3，可将计算得到的有限秩核 OT 目标与 H^k 度量下连续二次代价 OT 之间的差距界定为四个可解释项：核代价失配、熵正则、覆盖误差和离散化误差（附录 B.6 推论 1）。只有离散化项会随投影秩 N 增大而消失，其速率为 N^{−2(α−k)/d}，由正则性差距 α−k 决定；这是第 4 节经验分辨率不变性的形式化对应结果。失配项只取决于核的设计（线性核下为零；RBF 核下有限，并由数据尺度和带宽 σ 控制）。该定理依赖紧 Sobolev 嵌入[12]；本文光滑 PDE 基准满足这一条件[4]。

<span id="S040"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S040

**Original:** 4 Experiments

**中文:** 4 实验

<span id="S041"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S041

**Original:** Datasets. We use two classes of data. Sequence datasets: AEMET [15], gene expression [36], and the economics dataset [3] from Kerrigan et al. [25], plus financial time series from the Heston stochastic volatility model [21], treated as realizations of measures on path space C_p(R). PDE snapshots: the 1D Korteweg–de Vries (KdV) equation, the incompressible 2D Navier–Stokes equation [33], and their stochastic counterparts [43], treated as measures on Sobolev space H^k(Ω); Section 4.2 adds a turbulent 2D Navier–Stokes benchmark. Baselines. Denoising Diffusion Operator (DDO) with NCSN noise scale [34], functional DDPM [24], Generative Adversarial Neural Operator (GANO) [40], and original FFM [25]. We also include direct finite-dimensional OT baseline CFM-OT(L2) [48], which uses the same minibatch Sinkhorn solver and FNO backbone as our method but applies entropic OT to flattened grid vectors with unbounded L2 cost and white-noise base of finite-dimensional flow matching; kFFM’s Euclidean–RBF configuration instead uses bounded RBF cost on Euclidean distances. kFFM variants are named by cost, kFFM-Sig (signature), kFFM-Sob (Sobolev-RBF), kFFM-RBF (Euclidean–RBF), kFFM-Euc (raw L2), and tagged by base measure, gp (GP prior) or wn (white noise). Datasets, architectures, and training budgets, identical across methods, are detailed in Appendix C.

**中文:** 数据集。本文使用两类数据。序列数据集包括 AEMET[15]、基因表达数据[36]、Kerrigan 等人[25]使用的经济数据集[3]，以及来自 Heston 随机波动率模型[21]的金融时间序列；这些数据均视为路径空间 C_p(R) 上测度的实现。PDE 快照包括一维 Korteweg–de Vries（KdV）方程、不可压二维 Navier–Stokes 方程[33]及其随机版本[43]，并视为 Sobolev 空间 H^k(Ω) 上的测度；第 4.2 节另加入湍流二维 Navier–Stokes 基准。基线。采用带 NCSN 噪声尺度的去噪扩散算子（DDO）[34]、函数型 DDPM[24]、生成对抗神经算子（GANO）[40]和原始 FFM[25]。此外，还包含直接有限维 OT 基线 CFM-OT(L2)[48]：它与本文方法使用相同的小批次 Sinkhorn 求解器和 FNO 骨干，但在展平网格向量上使用无界 L2 代价执行熵正则 OT，并采用有限维流匹配的白噪声基础分布；kFFM 的 Euclidean–RBF 配置则使用定义在欧几里得距离上的有界 RBF 代价。kFFM 变体按代价命名：kFFM-Sig（signature）、kFFM-Sob（Sobolev-RBF）、kFFM-RBF（Euclidean–RBF）、kFFM-Euc（原始 L2）；同时按基础测度标注 gp（GP 先验）或 wn（白噪声）。各方法使用的数据集、架构和训练预算保持一致，详见附录 C。

**Original:** Metrics. The maximum mean discrepancy with an RBF kernel (MMD-RBF) is the primary population-level metric, computed with the unbiased estimator (so small negative values occur). To avoid judging an RBF-type coupling by an RBF-MMD alone, we also report non-kernel metrics (sliced Wasserstein distance and marginal W_1), the pointwise diagnostics of Kerrigan et al. [25] (autocorrelation error on sequences, log-spectral distance on PDEs, mean/variance summaries in Appendix C), and, on Navier–Stokes, physics diagnostics that capture non-Gaussian structure: enstrophy-distribution W_1, vorticity-PDF W_1, skewness and excess-kurtosis errors of pooled vorticity, and log-spectral error. Results aggregate 10 seeds (20 where marked); all methods share seeds, so tests are paired by seed.

**中文:** 指标。主要总体分布指标为基于 RBF 核的最大均值差异（MMD-RBF），采用无偏估计计算（因此可能出现微小负值）。为避免仅凭 RBF-MMD 评价 RBF 类耦合，本文还报告非核指标（切片 Wasserstein 距离和边缘 W_1）、逐点诊断指标[25]（序列的自相关误差、PDE 的对数谱距离、附录 C 中的均值/方差汇总）；对于 Navier–Stokes，还报告刻画非高斯结构的物理指标：涡量平方积分分布 W_1、涡量概率密度函数 W_1、合并涡量的偏度和超额峰度误差，以及对数谱误差。结果汇总 10 个随机种子（另有标明的 20 个）；所有方法共享随机种子，检验按种子配对。

<span id="S043"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S043

**Original:** 4.1 Distributional Quality

**中文:** 4.1 分布质量

<span id="S044"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S044

**Original:** Table 1 reports MMD-RBF against all baseline families. The “kFFM (selected)” column reports, per dataset, the configuration (OT cost and Gaussian base measure) selected on a held-out validation split by sliced Wasserstein distance, a non-kernel criterion (Appendix C.5); pointwise diagnostics by kernel variant appear in Appendix C.6. kFFM is the strongest method on every row, and the selected configurations follow a practitioner-usable pattern: the signature kernel with a GP base on path-space datasets, where function-space geometry matters most (except short-horizon Heston, where the top configurations are statistically indistinguishable and the rule picks the bounded RBF cost with a white-noise base); the Sobolev-RBF cost with a GP base on the KdV family; and the bounded Euclidean–RBF cost with a white-noise base on the Navier–Stokes family, whose rough fields are poorly matched by a smooth GP prior.

**中文:** 表 1 报告所有基线类别的 MMD-RBF。“kFFM（选定）”列针对每个数据集给出依据非核指标切片 Wasserstein 距离、在留出验证集上选出的配置（OT 代价和高斯基础测度；附录 C.5）；不同核变体的逐点诊断见附录 C.6。kFFM 在每一行均为最强方法，选出的配置呈现出便于实践使用的规律：路径数据集（函数空间几何最重要）使用以 GP 为基础测度的 signature 核，短时间范围 Heston 例外（最优配置统计上无法区分，因此规则选取有界 RBF 代价和白噪声基础测度）；KdV 系列使用以 GP 为基础测度的 Sobolev-RBF 代价；Navier–Stokes 系列使用有界欧几里得–RBF 代价和白噪声基础测度，因为光滑 GP 先验与粗糙场匹配较差。

<span id="S045"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S045

**Original:** Uncertainty and paired tests. Since unbiased MMD values can be tiny or negative, percentage gains are uninformative near the noise floor. Table 2 therefore reports per-seed mean±std and seed-paired tests against FFM at matched architecture and training budget: kFFM improves on FFM on every dataset at level 0.05 under both a paired t-test and a Wilcoxon signed-rank test, and wins on every shared seed for the four largest-gap datasets. In the GP-base rows only the coupling changes; in the white-noise rows (Heston, Navier–Stokes family) the base measure changes too, and at that base the effect of the kernel cost is isolated by comparison against CFM-OT(L2) in Table 1.

**中文:** 不确定性与配对检验。无偏 MMD 值可能很小或为负，因此在噪声底附近百分比增益缺乏解释意义。表 2 报告逐种子的均值±标准差，并在架构和训练预算匹配时对 kFFM 与 FFM 做按种子配对检验：在每个数据集上，配对 t 检验和 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验均以 0.05 显著性水平表明 kFFM 优于 FFM；在差距最大的四个数据集上，kFFM 对所有共享种子均获胜。GP 基础测度对应的行只有耦合方式变化；白噪声行（Heston、Navier–Stokes 系列）还改变基础测度，此时可通过表 1 中与 CFM-OT(L2) 的比较单独考察核代价的作用。

<span id="S046"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S046

**Original:** Non-kernel metrics. The gains survive the non-kernel metrics (Table 3): on the same runs, kFFM improves sliced Wasserstein distance and marginal W_1 over FFM on every dataset, by 18–20% on Gene Expression and Economy, where each comparison is significant when paired by seed, and by 2.9–4.1× on Navier–Stokes and Stoch. NS; for the signature configurations the coupling and evaluation kernels already differ. The pointwise diagnostics also expose what MMD understates: CFM-OT(L2) buys its pointwise-marginal fit with 105× and 66× worse autocorrelation error than kFFM-Sig on Economy and Gene Expression (Table 9); function-level structure is where the kernel coupling is strongest.

**中文:** 非核指标。性能增益在非核指标下仍成立（表 3）：在相同实验运行上，所有数据集的切片 Wasserstein 距离和边缘 W_1 均优于 FFM；Gene Expression 和 Economy 上提升 18–20%，且按随机种子配对的每项比较均有统计显著性；Navier–Stokes 和随机 Navier–Stokes（Stoch. NS）上提升 2.9–4.1 倍。对于 signature 配置，耦合核与评估核本身不同。逐点诊断还揭示 MMD 未充分反映的问题：CFM-OT(L2)改善逐点边缘拟合的代价是，Economy 和 Gene Expression 上的自相关误差分别比 kFFM-Sig 高 105 倍和 66 倍（表 9）；核耦合最突出的优势在函数级结构。

**Source:** p.8 S050

**Original:** Kernel choice and sensitivity. Re-aggregating the one-at-a-time sweeps of Appendix C.16: results are indistinguishable within seed-to-seed variation for RBF bandwidths σ∈[0.1,2] on every dataset, flat across three orders of magnitude of the Sinkhorn regularization (ε∈[0.001,1]) for bounded costs (the raw L2 cost degrades by ≈10× at ε≥0.5 on long volatility paths), and unchanged across signature hyperparameters (dyadic order 0–3, static bandwidth 0.1–5); over batch sizes b∈{64,...,512}, kFFM tracks FFM across the 8× range (Appendix C.8). The kernel family is the only choice that matters, and the rule we recommend is simple and consistent with the theory: match the kernel to the data class (signature for path-like data; Sobolev or Euclidean RBF for smooth fields, where Euclidean RBF is a reasonable default; RBF or L2 on very long paths, where signature-kernel memory grows with path length), set bandwidth by the median heuristic, and validate with a non-kernel metric on a held-out split (Appendix C.5). The properties the theorems require (boundedness, characteristicness) hold for every kernel considered, so theory constrains the family only weakly and the selection rule does the rest.

**中文:** 核选择与敏感性。重新汇总附录 C.16 的单因素扫描结果：在所有数据集上，当 RBF 带宽 σ∈[0.1,2] 时，结果差异均处于种子间波动范围内；对于有界代价，Sinkhorn 正则系数跨越三个数量级（ε∈[0.001,1]）时性能基本不变（长波动率路径上，原始 L2 代价在 ε≥0.5 时性能约恶化 10 倍）；signature 超参数（dyadic 阶数 0–3、静态带宽 0.1–5）变化也不影响结果。在批大小 b∈{64,...,512} 范围内，批次扩大 8 倍时 kFFM 与 FFM 的表现保持一致（附录 C.8）。唯一重要的选择是核族。本文建议的规则简单且与理论一致：按数据类别匹配核（路径类数据使用 signature；光滑场使用 Sobolev 或欧几里得 RBF，其中欧几里得 RBF 是合理默认选项；对于极长路径，signature 核内存会随路径长度增长，可使用 RBF 或 L2），用中位数启发式确定带宽，并在留出集上以非核指标进行验证（附录 C.5）。定理所需的性质（有界性、特征性）在本文考虑的所有核上都成立，因此理论对核族的限制较弱，剩余选择由上述规则决定。

<span id="S054"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S054

**Original:** (ii) OT-coupled flow matching is far more robust than the score-based and diffusion alternatives: 23–32× better vorticity-PDF W1, 5.7–6.9× better enstrophy W1, and 17–43× better vorticity-kurtosis error (Figure 1). (iii) Against FFM run with its GP prior, kFFM improves every metric except kurtosis (a tie), with seed-paired significance (MMD 73× lower, p=3×10⁻⁴; sliced-W 3.1×, p=1.3×10⁻⁶; log-spectrum 3.2×, p=0.005; vorticity-PDF 2.9×, p=0.007; skewness 18×, p=0.016). This gain combines the base-measure change and the coupling: the three GP-base arms are indistinguishable, so in this regime the white-noise base does most of the work, and at that base the kernel and raw-L2 costs tie, as in (i). All methods, ours included, still leave headroom on sliced-W in this regime (Section 6).

**中文:** （ii）OT 耦合流匹配比基于得分和扩散的方法稳健得多：涡量 PDF 的 W₁ 改善 23–32 倍，enstrophy 的 W₁ 改善 5.7–6.9 倍，涡量峰度误差改善 17–43 倍（图 1）。（iii）相对于使用 GP 先验运行的 FFM，kFFM 除峰度（并列）外在所有指标上均有改善，且按随机种子配对后具有统计显著性（MMD 低 73 倍，p=3×10⁻⁴；切片 W 改善 3.1 倍，p=1.3×10⁻⁶；对数谱改善 3.2 倍，p=0.005；涡量 PDF 改善 2.9 倍，p=0.007；偏度改善 18 倍，p=0.016）。增益同时来自基础测度和耦合方式的变化：三个 GP 基础测度方案无法区分，因此该区域的主要贡献来自白噪声基础测度；在该基础测度下，核代价与原始 L2 代价打平，与（i）一致。包括本文方法在内，所有方法在此区域的切片 W 指标上仍有改进空间（第 6 节）。

**Original:** 4.3 Cost, Efficiency, and Convergence

<span id="S056"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S056

**Original:** Compute. Measured on every benchmark (Appendix C.9), the end-to-end training overhead of the per-minibatch Sinkhorn solve over FFM is 5–7% at the largest resolution we train (128² Navier–Stokes); the O(b²) coupling cost is independent of model size and resolution, so its relative weight

**中文:** 计算开销。在所有基准上进行测量（附录 C.9）后发现，相对于 FFM，每小批次执行 Sinkhorn 求解带来的端到端训练开销，在本文训练的最大分辨率（128² Navier–Stokes）下为 5–7%；耦合计算的 O(b²) 代价与模型大小和分辨率无关，因此其相对占比


## p.10 <span id="page-10"></span>

<span id="S057"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S057

**Original:** shrinks as the backbone grows. Peak coupling memory is 80–151 MB (1.5–9% of the FNO forward-plus-backward peak), and per-batch coupling time is comparable to one FNO step. The bounded RBF cost is about 8× faster than unbounded-L2 Sinkhorn at b=512 and trains 15–25% faster end-to-end, the conditioning effect of Theorem 1; giving FFM the extra budget as additional epochs does not close the gap.

**中文:** 随着骨干网络规模增大，耦合开销占比会降低。耦合所需峰值内存为 80–151 MB（FNO 前向与反向峰值内存的 1.5–9%），每批次耦合耗时与一次 FNO 更新相当。在 b=512 时，有界 RBF 代价的 Sinkhorn 计算速度约为无界 L2 Sinkhorn 的 8 倍，端到端训练快 15–25%，体现了定理 1 所述的条件改善；即使把额外训练预算用于增加 FFM 训练轮数，也无法缩小这一差距。

<span id="S058"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S058

**Original:** No straightness claim. We claim no straightness theorem: Appendix C.14 (Proposition 6, Corollary 2) shows that kFFM changes only the endpoint coupling while the affine interpolation template is fixed, and Hilbert-space Gaussian geometry does not inherit the Euclidean straightness picture that motivates finite-dimensional OT-CFM [52]. We therefore make no efficiency claim; Appendix C.15 reports only training-time convergence of the target metric, where kFFM ends training at a better MMD-RBF than FFM on every dataset.

**中文:** 不声称路径更直。本文不提出路径直线性的定理：附录 C.14（命题 6、推论 2）表明，kFFM 只改变端点耦合，仿射插值模板保持固定；Hilbert 空间的高斯几何也不继承有限维 OT-CFM[52]所依赖的欧几里得直线性图景。因此，本文不提出效率优势的主张；附录 C.15 仅报告目标指标随训练时间的收敛情况，其中 kFFM 在所有数据集上训练结束时的 MMD-RBF 均优于 FFM。

<span id="S059"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S059

**Original:** 5 Related Work

**中文:** 5 相关工作

<span id="S060"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S060

**Original:** Minibatch OT coupling for flow matching was introduced by Tong et al. [48] and Pooladian et al. [39], with follow-ups refining the transport objective [28,51] and analyses of minibatch plans [13,14]; all assume R^d sample spaces. In function space, FFM [25] lifts flow matching to Hilbert spaces via Gaussian measures, alongside functional diffusion [24,34] and adversarial neural operators [40]. Concurrent function-space flow matching changes the objective, the task, or the prior, but not the coupling: Functional Rectified Flow [53] straightens flows by re-training on model-generated endpoint pairs across training rounds, a mechanism complementary to and composable with our within-batch, data-geometry coupling; Operator Flow Matching [30] and flow matching with GP priors [27] target conditional time-series forecasting with an independent prior-data coupling. Kernel embeddings of measures [20,46] underlie MMD and two-sample testing, recently on path space with the signature kernel [6]; Sinkhorn divergences interpolate between OT and MMD [16], and the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence [31] kernelizes them. To our knowledge, ours is the first work to perform OT endpoint coupling in function-space flow matching; Appendix D gives an extended discussion, including MMD gradient flows.

**中文:** 流匹配中的小批次 OT 耦合由 Tong 等人[48]和 Pooladian 等人[39]提出，后续工作改进了传输目标[28,51]并分析了小批次计划[13,14]；这些方法均假设样本空间为 R^d。在函数空间中，FFM[25]通过高斯测度将流匹配提升到 Hilbert 空间，此外还有函数扩散方法[24,34]和对抗神经算子[40]。同期的函数空间流匹配工作改变了目标函数、任务或先验，但没有改变耦合方式：Functional Rectified Flow[53]在多轮训练中使用模型生成的端点对重新训练，从而使流变直；这一机制与本文在批次内采用的数据几何耦合互补且可组合。Operator Flow Matching[30]以及带 GP 先验的流匹配[27]针对条件时间序列预测，仍采用先验与数据独立耦合。测度的核嵌入[20,46]是 MMD 和双样本检验的基础，近期也使用 signature 核扩展到路径空间[6]；Sinkhorn 散度在 OT 与 MMD 之间插值[16]，Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度[31]则将其核化。据作者所知，本文是首个在函数空间流匹配中执行 OT 端点耦合的工作；附录 D 进一步讨论相关工作，包括 MMD 梯度流。

<span id="S061"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S061

**Original:** 6 Conclusion and Limitations

**中文:** 6 结论与局限

<span id="S062"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S062

**Original:** kFFM replaces FFM’s independent endpoint pairing with geometry-aware kernel-OT coupling while keeping the neural-operator backbone. Theoretically, the kernel-OT surrogate is uniformly bounded and well-posed on Banach spaces, admits an explicit error decomposition against quadratic-cost OT that isolates an irreducible kernel-cost mismatch, and is discretization-invariant at rate N^{−2(α−k)/d}. Empirically, kFFM improves distributional matching on time-series and PDE benchmarks with paired-seed significance, and the gains survive non-kernel metrics, physics diagnostics, and a turbulent Navier–Stokes stress test, at a training overhead of 5–7% at the largest resolution we train.

**中文:** kFFM 在保持神经算子骨干不变的同时，将 FFM 的端点独立配对替换为几何感知的核 OT 耦合。理论上，核 OT 替代目标在 Banach 空间上具有一致有界性和良定性；相对于二次代价 OT，可得到分离出不可约核代价失配项的显式误差分解；其离散化不变性速率为 N^{−2(α−k)/d}。实验上，kFFM 在时间序列和 PDE 基准上的分布匹配性能得到提升，配对随机种子检验具有统计显著性；该增益在非核指标、物理诊断和 Navier–Stokes 湍流压力测试下仍成立，最大分辨率下训练开销为 5–7%。

<span id="S063"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S063

**Original:** Limitations. Several deserve plain statement. First, our theory concerns the surrogate coupling: it certifies well-posedness, an error decomposition, and discretization invariance, but says nothing about straightness or sampling efficiency in function space, on which we make no claim, and it does not by itself explain the sample-quality gains, which remain an empirical finding. Second, the theory is least informative in low-regularity regimes: on the turbulent benchmark the prior-data L2 distances more than double relative to the standard one (median 91 vs. 40), pushing the RBF cost into saturation, where Δ_κ grows and the covering term of Theorem 2 is large for any practical ε; there the empirical results carry the method. Third, turbulent small-scale statistics remain an open problem for function-space generative models. On current evidence the failure sits mainly with score-based and diffusion baselines (vorticity-kurtosis errors of 1.3–3.4 and enstrophy W_1≈1), but all methods, ours included, leave headroom on sliced-W (0.19 for the best method), and in this regime the white-noise base rather than the coupling does most of the work; OT-coupled flow matching is currently the strongest option. Finally, signature-kernel memory grows with path length (0.63 GB at 100 time points vs. 12.5 GB at 512), so very long paths fall back to RBF or L2 costs. Future work includes stronger function-space OT formulations [28,52] and base measures and costs designed for rough, turbulent data.

**中文:** 局限。以下几点需要明确说明。第一，本文理论针对的是替代耦合：它证明良定性、误差分解和离散化不变性，但不涉及函数空间中的路径直线性或采样效率，本文也不对此作主张；理论本身亦不能解释样本质量提升，这仍是实验发现。第二，理论在低正则性区域的解释力最弱：在湍流基准上，先验与数据之间的 L2 距离中位数超过标准基准的两倍（91 对 40），使 RBF 代价进入饱和区，此时 Δ_κ 增大，且定理 2 的覆盖项在任何实际 ε 下都较大；该区域的方法表现主要由实验结果支撑。第三，函数空间生成模型对湍流小尺度统计的刻画仍是开放问题。现有证据显示，问题主要出现在基于得分和扩散的基线上（涡量峰度误差为 1.3–3.4，enstrophy W₁≈1）；但所有方法（包括本文方法）在切片 W 上仍有提升空间（最佳方法为 0.19）。在这一设置下，白噪声基础测度比耦合方式贡献更大；目前 OT 耦合流匹配是最强选项。最后，signature 核的内存随路径长度增加（100 个时间点需要 0.63 GB，而 512 个点需要 12.5 GB），因此极长路径需退回 RBF 或 L2 代价。未来工作包括更强的函数空间 OT 表述[28,52]，以及面向粗糙、湍流数据设计的基础测度和代价。
