**Original:** A Additional Technical Background

**中文:** A 补充技术背景

<span id="S127"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S127

**Original:** This appendix collects the mathematical background on which the paper relies: probability measures on function spaces, reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces and kernel embeddings, path signatures, and optimal transport.

**中文:** 本附录汇总本文所依赖的数学背景：函数空间上的概率测度、再生核 Hilbert 空间与核嵌入、路径 signature，以及最优传输。

<span id="S128"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S128

**Original:** A.1 Probability Measure in Function Space

**中文:** A.1 函数空间中的概率测度

<span id="S129"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S129

**Original:** In this section we provide definitions and necessary theoretical results for probability measures in function space, highlighting general analytical difficulties. We give high-level intuition and omit proofs; for details see Rudin [41] and Da Prato [8]. Definition 3 (Hilbert Space). A Hilbert space is an inner-product space complete in the norm induced by its inner product, defined as ⟨·,·⟩:H×H→F, where F=R for a real Hilbert space. The inner product gives H the norm ||x||:=√⟨x,x⟩ and metric d(x,y)=||x−y|| for all x,y∈H. Let B(x,r) be open balls under d. The Borel σ-algebra B(H) is generated by these balls: B(H):=σ({B(x,r):x∈H,r>0}).

**中文:** 本节给出函数空间中概率测度的定义和必要理论结果，并突出一般情况下的分析难点。本文仅提供高层直觉而省略证明；详细内容参见 Rudin[41]和 Da Prato[8]。定义 3（Hilbert 空间）。Hilbert 空间是一个内积空间，且在内积诱导的范数下完备；其内积写作 ⟨·,·⟩:H×H→F，实 Hilbert 空间中 F=R。内积为 H 定义范数 ||x||:=√⟨x,x⟩ 及度量 d(x,y)=||x−y||（对所有 x,y∈H）。令 B(x,r) 表示度量 d 下的开球。由这些开球生成的 Borel σ-代数记为 B(H)：B(H):=σ({B(x,r):x∈H,r>0})。

<span id="S130"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S130

**Original:** Another desirable property of Hilbert spaces is the eigenfunction spectral decomposition for bounded linear operators. Theorem 4. Let T:H→H be a compact, self-adjoint, bounded linear operator. There exists an orthonormal set of eigenvectors {e_n}_{n∈I} and real eigenvalues {λ_n}_{n∈I} such that: (1) Te_n=λ_ne_n; (2) λ_n→0 if I is infinite; (3) each nonzero eigenvalue has finite multiplicity; (4) the closure of the span of nonzero eigenvectors equals the closure of Ran(T); (5) Tx=Σ_{i∈I}λ_i⟨x,e_i⟩e_i for x∈H.

**中文:** Hilbert 空间的另一项理想性质是有界线性算子的特征函数谱分解。定理 4。设 T:H→H 为紧、自伴的有界线性算子，则存在一组正交归一特征向量 {e_n}_{n∈I} 和实特征值 {λ_n}_{n∈I}，满足：（1）Te_n=λ_ne_n；（2）若 I 无限，则 λ_n→0；（3）每个非零特征值的重数有限；（4）非零特征向量张成空间的闭包等于 Ran(T) 的闭包；（5）对 x∈H，有 Tx=Σ_{i∈I}λ_i⟨x,e_i⟩e_i。

<span id="S131"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S131

**Original:** The main text adopts a measure-theoretic view of probability, which is crucial for function spaces. This follows from the result known as “no Lebesgue measure in infinite dimensions”: if X is an infinite-dimensional separable normed linear space, there is no nontrivial, translation-invariant, σ-additive Borel measure μ on X that is finite on every nonempty open ball [50]. Thus there is no universal definition of probability density as in R^d; between two absolutely continuous measures only the more general Radon–Nikodym derivative is defined. Definition 4 (Singular and Absolute Continuity). On a measurable space (Ω,F), measures μ,ν are mutually singular, written μ⊥ν, if there exists A∈F such that μ(A)=0 and ν(Ω\A)=0. μ is absolutely continuous with respect to ν, written μ≪ν, if ν(A)=0 implies μ(A)=0. If μ≪ν and ν≪μ, then μ∼ν and the measures are equivalent. Definition 5 (Radon–Nikodym Derivative). If μ≪ν and μ,ν are σ-finite, then there exists a measurable f:Ω→[0,∞] such that for all A∈F, μ(A)=∫_A f dν; f is the Radon–Nikodym derivative of μ with respect to ν, denoted dμ/dν. On (R^d,B(R^d)) with ν Lebesgue measure, f is also called the probability density function of μ.

**中文:** 正文采用测度论的概率观点，这对函数空间至关重要。其依据是“无限维空间不存在 Lebesgue 测度”这一结果：若 X 是无限维可分赋范线性空间，则 X 上不存在一种非平凡、平移不变、σ-可加的 Borel 测度 μ，使其在每个非空开球上的测度都有限[50]。因此，函数空间中不存在类似 R^d 情形的通用概率密度定义；对于绝对连续的两个测度，只能定义更一般的 Radon–Nikodym 导数。定义 4（奇异与绝对连续）。在可测空间 (Ω,F) 上，若存在 A∈F 使 μ(A)=0 且 ν(Ω\A)=0，则称 μ、ν 互相奇异，记作 μ⊥ν。若 ν(A)=0 蕴含 μ(A)=0，则称 μ 关于 ν 绝对连续，记作 μ≪ν。若 μ≪ν 且 ν≪μ，则记 μ∼ν，称两个测度等价。定义 5（Radon–Nikodym 导数）。若 μ≪ν 且 μ、ν 均为 σ-有限测度，则存在可测函数 f:Ω→[0,∞]，使得对任意 A∈F，μ(A)=∫_A f dν；f 称为 μ 关于 ν 的 Radon–Nikodym 导数，记作 dμ/dν。在 (R^d,B(R^d)) 上若 ν 为 Lebesgue 测度，f 也称为 μ 的概率密度函数。



## p.17 <span id="page-17"></span>

<span id="S132"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S132

**Original:** Though there is no infinite-dimensional Lebesgue measure and hence no universal way to define density, the most feasible and well-understood way to have a Lebesgue-like measure is to define Gaussian measures. Definition 6 (Gaussian Measure). Let a∈H and Q∈L_+(H), the space of positive, symmetric, trace-class linear operators. The Gaussian measure μ:=N_{a,Q} on (H,B(H)) with mean a and covariance operator Q is defined by its Fourier transform: N̂_{a,Q}(h)=exp{i⟨a,h⟩−(1/2)⟨Qh,h⟩}.

**中文:** 尽管无限维空间不存在 Lebesgue 测度，因而也没有定义密度的通用方法，但构造类似 Lebesgue 测度的最可行且最成熟方式是定义高斯测度。定义 6（高斯测度）。设 a∈H，Q∈L_+(H)，其中 L_+(H) 表示正、对称、迹类线性算子构成的空间。定义 (H,B(H)) 上均值为 a、协方差算子为 Q 的高斯测度 μ:=N_{a,Q}，其 Fourier 变换为：N̂_{a,Q}(h)=exp{i⟨a,h⟩−(1/2)⟨Qh,h⟩}。

<span id="S133"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S133

**Original:** For every choice of a,Q, there exists a unique Gaussian measure. In Da Prato [8], Chapter 1, an interesting construction uses the theory of infinite product measures and the isomorphism between sequence space l² and Hilbert space H, mapping the projection defined through the spectral decomposition indices to Gaussian measures on R (the one-dimensional Gaussian distribution). Still, for one Gaussian measure to be absolutely continuous with respect to another, strong conditions on their means and covariance operators are required: the Feldman–Hájek theorem and, for a shifted mean, the Cameron–Martin formula. Definition 7 (Cameron–Martin Space). Let H be a Hilbert space and μ=N_{0,Q} a centered Gaussian measure. The Cameron–Martin space induced by N_{0,Q} is H_μ:=Ran(Q^{1/2}), with inner product ⟨h_1,h_2⟩:=⟨Q^{−1/2}h_1,Q^{−1/2}h_2⟩_H.

**中文:** 对任意 a、Q，均存在唯一的高斯测度。Da Prato[8]第 1 章给出一种有趣的构造方法：利用无限乘积测度理论，以及序列空间 l² 与 Hilbert 空间 H 之间的同构，将由谱分解指标定义的投影映射到 R 上的高斯测度（即一维高斯分布）。然而，要使一个高斯测度相对于另一个高斯测度绝对连续，其均值和协方差算子必须满足严格条件：即 Feldman–Hájek 定理；对于均值平移，还需 Cameron–Martin 公式。定义 7（Cameron–Martin 空间）。设 H 为 Hilbert 空间，μ=N_{0,Q} 为中心高斯测度，则其诱导的 Cameron–Martin 空间为 H_μ:=Ran(Q^{1/2})，内积定义为 ⟨h_1,h_2⟩:=⟨Q^{−1/2}h_1,Q^{−1/2}h_2⟩_H。

<span id="S134"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S134

**Original:** The general dichotomy for Gaussian measures is the Feldman–Hájek theorem. Theorem 5 (Feldman–Hájek). Let H be a separable Hilbert space and N_{a,P},N_{b,Q} two Gaussian measures. Then either N_{a,P}∼N_{b,Q} or N_{a,P}⊥N_{b,Q}, and N_{a,P}∼N_{b,Q} iff: (1) P^{1/2}(H)=Q^{1/2}(H)=:H_0; (2) a−b∈H_0; (3) (P^{−1/2}Q^{1/2})(P^{−1/2}Q^{1/2})*−I is Hilbert–Schmidt on H_0.

**中文:** 高斯测度的一般二分性质由 Feldman–Hájek 定理给出。定理 5（Feldman–Hájek）。设 H 为可分 Hilbert 空间，N_{a,P}、N_{b,Q} 为两个高斯测度，则它们要么等价（N_{a,P}∼N_{b,Q}），要么互相奇异（N_{a,P}⊥N_{b,Q}）；二者等价当且仅当：（1）P^{1/2}(H)=Q^{1/2}(H)=:H_0；（2）a−b∈H_0；（3）(P^{−1/2}Q^{1/2})(P^{−1/2}Q^{1/2})*−I 在 H_0 上为 Hilbert–Schmidt 算子。

<span id="S135"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S135

**Original:** Theorem 6 (Cameron–Martin Formula). Let μ=N_{0,Q}, ν=N_{a,Q} be two Gaussian measures. (1) If a∉Q^{1/2}(H), then μ⊥ν. (2) If a∈Q^{1/2}(H), then μ∼ν. (3) If μ∼ν, with h:=Q^{−1/2}a, then dν/dμ(x)=exp{ĥ(x)−(1/2)||h||²_H}, where ĥ denotes the Cameron–Martin linear functional associated with h. These are the conditions enforced on the FFM model [25], which relies on absolute continuity between the marginal and conditional measure at every time t in the probability flow.

**中文:** 定理 6（Cameron–Martin 公式）。设 μ=N_{0,Q}、ν=N_{a,Q} 为两个高斯测度。（1）若 a∉Q^{1/2}(H)，则 μ⊥ν。（2）若 a∈Q^{1/2}(H)，则 μ∼ν。（3）若 μ∼ν，令 h:=Q^{−1/2}a，则 dν/dμ(x)=exp{ĥ(x)−(1/2)||h||²_H}，其中 ĥ 表示与 h 关联的 Cameron–Martin 线性泛函。FFM 模型[25]施加了这些条件，因为其概率流要求每个时刻 t 的边缘测度与条件测度之间绝对连续。

<span id="S136"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S136

**Original:** For a general Banach space, which is a complete normed space but need not have inner-product structure or spectral decomposition, the analysis becomes more abstract. For instance, without a coordinate map, the preceding product-measure construction of Gaussian measures no longer applies directly. We refer readers to Ledoux and Talagrand [29] for this broader probability theory, and instead explore Hilbert-space embeddings of probability measures in Section A.3.

**中文:** 对于一般 Banach 空间（即完备赋范空间，但不一定具有内积结构或谱分解），分析会更加抽象。例如，没有坐标映射时，前述通过乘积测度构造高斯测度的方法不再能直接应用。更广泛的概率理论可参见 Ledoux 和 Talagrand[29]；本文则在 A.3 节转而讨论概率测度的 Hilbert 空间嵌入。

<span id="S137"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S137

**Original:** A.2 Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space

**中文:** A.2 再生核 Hilbert 空间

<span id="S138"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S138

**Original:** A Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space is a Hilbert space of functions with a particular structure that makes each point evaluation well-behaved.

**中文:** 再生核 Hilbert 空间（RKHS）是一类具有特定结构的函数 Hilbert 空间，该结构使每个点上的函数求值都具有良好性质。



## p.18 <span id="page-18"></span>

<span id="S139"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S139

**Original:** Definition 8 (Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space). Let X be a non-empty set. A Hilbert space H of functions f:X→R is a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space if for every x∈X the point-evaluation functional δ_x:H→R, defined by δ_x(f)=f(x), is continuous and bounded. By the Riesz representation theorem, there exists a unique element κ_x∈H such that f(x)=⟨f,κ_x⟩_H for all f∈H. This is the reproducing property that defines an RKHS. Since κ_x is both a function on X and an element of H, κ_x(y)=δ_y(κ_x)=⟨κ_x,κ_y⟩_H, where κ_y∈H is associated with δ_y. This lets us define the reproducing kernel κ(x,y):=⟨κ_x,κ_y⟩_H. It follows that κ:X×X→R is symmetric and positive definite.

**中文:** 定义 8（再生核 Hilbert 空间）。设 X 为非空集合。若函数 f:X→R 构成的 Hilbert 空间 H 满足：对每个 x∈X，点求值泛函 δ_x:H→R（定义为 δ_x(f)=f(x)）连续且有界，则 H 称为再生核 Hilbert 空间。根据 Riesz 表示定理，存在唯一元素 κ_x∈H，使得对所有 f∈H，f(x)=⟨f,κ_x⟩_H。这一性质称为再生性质，是 RKHS 的定义特征。由于 κ_x 既是定义在 X 上的函数，也是 H 的元素，因此 κ_x(y)=δ_y(κ_x)=⟨κ_x,κ_y⟩_H，其中 κ_y∈H 与 δ_y 对应。由此可定义再生核 κ(x,y):=⟨κ_x,κ_y⟩_H。容易看出，κ:X×X→R 对称且正定。

<span id="S140"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S140

**Original:** A core reason to use kernels is that they implicitly define feature maps in high, potentially infinite-dimensional spaces, known as the “kernel trick.” Definition 9 (Canonical Feature Maps). Let κ be a kernel with RKHS H_κ. The canonical feature map φ:X→H_κ is φ(x)=κ(·,x), and it satisfies κ(x,y)=⟨φ(x),φ(y)⟩, the kernel trick. Thus inner products in feature space can be computed without explicitly constructing that space. In this work, the cost function in Proposition 3 can be understood through this kernel trick.

**中文:** 使用核的核心原因之一，是核能够隐式定义到高维乃至无限维空间的特征映射，这通常称为“核技巧”。定义 9（规范特征映射）。设 κ 为核，其 RKHS 为 H_κ。规范特征映射 φ:X→H_κ 定义为 φ(x)=κ(·,x)，并满足基本恒等式 κ(x,y)=⟨φ(x),φ(y)⟩，即核技巧。因此，无需显式构造特征空间即可计算其中的内积。在本文中，可利用核技巧理解命题 3 的代价函数。

<span id="S141"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S141

**Original:** For some kernel choices, it is useful to know when the RKHS is rich enough to approximate arbitrary continuous functions and when the induced mean embedding distinguishes probability distributions. Kernels with the former property are “universal.” Definition 10 (Universal Kernel). Let X be a compact metric space. A continuous kernel on X is universal if its associated RKHS H_κ is dense in C(X) under the supremum norm ||·||_∞; that is, for any f∈C(X) and any ε>0, there exists g∈H_κ such that sup_{x∈X}|f(x)−g(x)|<ε.

**中文:** 对于某些核选择，需要了解其 RKHS 是否足够丰富，能够逼近任意连续函数，以及诱导的均值嵌入是否能够区分概率分布。具有前一种性质的核称为“通用核”。定义 10（通用核）。设 X 为紧度量空间。若定义在 X 上的连续核所对应的 RKHS H_κ 关于上确界范数 ||·||_∞ 在 C(X) 中稠密，则称该核为通用核；也就是说，对任意 f∈C(X) 和 ε>0，都存在 g∈H_κ，使得 sup_{x∈X}|f(x)−g(x)|<ε。

<span id="S142"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S142

**Original:** Universality ensures that a kernel can approximate continuous functions, whereas characteristicness ensures that its mean embedding distinguishes probability distributions. A kernel with the latter property is characteristic. Definition 11 (Characteristic Kernel). Let X be a compact metric space. A kernel κ on X is characteristic if the mean-embedding map μ↦∫_Xκ(·,x)dμ(x) is injective on P(X). Equivalently, if ∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)−2∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dν(y)+∫∫κ(x,y)dν(x)dν(y)=0, then μ=ν.

**中文:** 通用性保证核能够逼近连续函数；特征性则保证其均值嵌入能够区分概率分布。具有后一性质的核称为“特征核”。定义 11（特征核）。设 X 为紧度量空间。若核 κ 的均值嵌入映射 μ↦∫_Xκ(·,x)dμ(x) 在概率测度空间 P(X) 上为单射，则称 κ 为特征核。等价地，若 ∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)−2∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dν(y)+∫∫κ(x,y)dν(x)dν(y)=0，则必有 μ=ν。



## p.19 <span id="page-19"></span>

<span id="S143"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S143

**Original:** Universal kernels on compact spaces are characteristic, but the converse does not hold in general. These properties help interpret kernel discrepancies: characteristicness makes the kernel mean embedding injective, while universality gives a stronger approximation property on compact sets. The main theorems in Section 3.2 require only the stated assumptions: bounded positive-definite kernels, compactness for the error decomposition, and Sobolev regularity for discretization invariance.

**中文:** 紧空间上的通用核具有特征性，但反向结论一般不成立。这些性质有助于理解核差异：特征性保证核均值嵌入为单射，而通用性则在紧集上提供更强的逼近性质。第 3.2 节的主要定理只要求其中明确陈述的假设：有界正定核、误差分解所需的紧性，以及离散化不变性所需的 Sobolev 正则性。

<span id="S144"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S144

**Original:** The radial basis function kernel is used in our Sobolev-space experiments. On a Sobolev space H^k(Ω), it is κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=exp{−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²)}, where σ>0 is the bandwidth. This Gaussian kernel is symmetric, positive definite, and characteristic on separable Hilbert spaces [54]. It is also bounded, κ_RBF^(k)(f,f)=1, satisfying the boundedness condition used in Theorems 1 and 2.

**中文:** 本文在 Sobolev 空间实验中使用径向基函数（RBF）核。在 Sobolev 空间 H^k(Ω) 上，其定义为 κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=exp{−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²)}，其中 σ>0 为带宽参数。该高斯核在可分 Hilbert 空间上对称、正定且具有特征性[54]。此外，该核有界，κ_RBF^(k)(f,f)=1，满足定理 1 和定理 2 中的有界性条件。

<span id="S145"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S145

**Original:** A.3 Kernel Embedding of Probability Measure

**中文:** A.3 概率测度的核嵌入

<span id="S146"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S146

**Original:** We next consider embedding probability measures into an RKHS, following the framework developed by Sriperumbudur et al. [46]. This is central to our approach because it provides a tractable way to define kernel-induced transport costs on function spaces. Infinite-dimensional spaces lack a Lebesgue-like reference measure, making density-based approaches unavailable. The key idea is to embed probability measures as elements of an RKHS, where distances between distributions can be computed without explicit density estimation. For the embedding to be well-defined, an integrability condition on the kernel is required. Proposition 1. Let κ be a measurable kernel on X. Then ∫_X√κ(x,x)dP(x)<∞ for every P∈P(X) if and only if κ is bounded.

**中文:** 接下来，按照 Sriperumbudur 等人[46]建立的框架，讨论将概率测度嵌入 RKHS。这是本文方法的核心，因为它提供了一种可计算的方式来定义函数空间上的核诱导传输代价。无限维空间缺少类似 Lebesgue 测度的参考测度，因此无法采用基于密度的方法。关键思路是将概率测度嵌入 RKHS，使我们无需显式估计密度即可计算分布间距离。为保证嵌入定义良好，需要满足核的可积性条件。命题 1。设 κ 是定义在 X 上的可测核，则对每个 P∈P(X)，∫_X√κ(x,x)dP(x)<∞，当且仅当 κ 有界。

<span id="S147"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S147

**Original:** With the mean embedding, define a natural distance between probability measures using a characteristic kernel (Definition 11). The metric γ_κ satisfies γ_κ(P,Q)=0 iff P=Q, for P,Q∈P. Theorem 7 (Theorem 1 in Sriperumbudur et al. [46]). Let P_κ:={P∈P_M:∫_M√κ(x,x)dP(x)<∞}, where κ is measurable on M. For any P,Q∈P_κ, γ_κ(P,Q):=||∫_Mκ(·,x)dP(x)−∫_Mκ(·,x)dQ(x)||_{H_κ}=||P_κ−Q_κ||_{H_κ}.

**中文:** 利用均值嵌入，可通过特征核（定义 11）定义概率测度之间的自然距离。对 P,Q∈P，该度量 γ_κ 满足 γ_κ(P,Q)=0 当且仅当 P=Q。定理 7（Sriperumbudur 等人[46]的定理 1）。令 P_κ:={P∈P_M:∫_M√κ(x,x)dP(x)<∞}，其中 κ 是定义在 M 上的可测核。对任意 P,Q∈P_κ，有 γ_κ(P,Q):=||∫_Mκ(·,x)dP(x)−∫_Mκ(·,x)dQ(x)||_{H_κ}=||P_κ−Q_κ||_{H_κ}。

<span id="S148"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S148

**Original:** Below we show that the bounded RBF kernel on a Sobolev space H^k is characteristic, providing additional support for the kernel choices in the main text. Lemma 1 (proved in Sriperumbudur et al. [46]). Let κ be an integrally strictly positive-definite kernel on a topological space M. Then κ is characteristic to P_M. Definition 12. Let M(H) be the finite signed Borel measures on H. A bounded measurable kernel κ on H is integrally strictly positive definite (ISPD) if for every μ∈M(H)\{0}, ∫_{H×H}κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)>0. Equivalently, if m_κ(μ):=∫_Hκ(·,x)dμ(x) is the kernel mean element (KME), then ∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)=||m_κ(μ)||²_{H_κ}.

**中文:** 下文证明定义在 Sobolev 空间 H^k 上的有界 RBF 核具有特征性，为正文中的核选择提供进一步依据。引理 1（证明见 Sriperumbudur 等人[46]）。设 κ 是拓扑空间 M 上积分严格正定的核，则 κ 对 P_M 具有特征性。定义 12。令 M(H) 表示 H 上有限符号 Borel 测度的集合。若有界可测核 κ 满足对所有 μ∈M(H)\{0}，∫_{H×H}κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)>0，则称 κ 为积分严格正定（ISPD）核。等价地，若 m_κ(μ):=∫_Hκ(·,x)dμ(x) 表示核均值元素（KME），则 ∫∫κ(x,y)dμ(x)dμ(y)=||m_κ(μ)||²_{H_κ}。



## p.20 <span id="page-20"></span>

<span id="S149"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S149

**Original:** One can interpret ISPD as the statement that a zero KME implies a zero measure: m_κ(μ)=0⇒μ=0. Theorem 8. Let H be a separable Hilbert space. The Gaussian kernel on H is ISPD with respect to M(H) [54]. This theorem and Lemma 1 imply that the RBF kernel is characteristic on the Sobolev spaces of interest.

**中文:** 可将 ISPD 理解为：核均值元素为零蕴含测度为零，即 m_κ(μ)=0⇒μ=0。定理 8。设 H 为可分 Hilbert 空间，则定义在 H 上的高斯核相对于 M(H) 为 ISPD[54]。由该定理和引理 1 可知，本文关注的 Sobolev 空间上的 RBF 核具有特征性。

<span id="S150"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S150

**Original:** A.4 Path Space and Signature Kernel

**中文:** A.4 路径空间与 signature 核

<span id="S151"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S151

**Original:** Denote by C_p the space of continuous paths x:[a,b]→V of finite p-variation (see Definition 1.1.4 in Cass and Salvi [5]), with p∈[1,2). V is a finite-dimensional Banach space. For time-series datasets we usually have x:[0,T]→R^d. For discrete observations, one may use piecewise-linear interpolation, which also has finite p-variation. Let T be the tensor algebra (written T((R^d)) in Section 2.3 when V=R^d): T:=∏_{m=0}^∞V^{⊗m}. Definition 13 (Signature transform). The signature transform S:C_p→T maps a path x to the tensor sequence S(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),S^(2)(x),...), where the mth tensor is the iterated integral S^(m)(x):=∫_{a≤t_1<···<t_m≤b}dx_{t_1}⊗···⊗dx_{t_m}∈V^{⊗m}, m∈N. Here ⊗ is the standard tensor product.

**中文:** 令 C_p 表示有限 p-变差连续路径 x:[a,b]→V 构成的空间（见 Cass 和 Salvi[5]定义 1.1.4），其中 p∈[1,2)，V 为有限维 Banach 空间。对于时间序列数据，通常有 x:[0,T]→R^d。对于离散观测，可采用分段线性插值，所得路径也具有有限 p-变差。令 T 表示张量代数（当 V=R^d 时，第 2.3 节记作 T((R^d))）：T:=∏_{m=0}^∞V^{⊗m}。定义 13（signature 变换）。signature 变换 S:C_p→T 将路径 x 映射为张量序列 S(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),S^(2)(x),...)；其中第 m 阶张量为迭代积分 S^(m)(x):=∫_{a≤t_1<···<t_m≤b}dx_{t_1}⊗···⊗dx_{t_m}∈V^{⊗m}，m∈N。这里 ⊗ 表示标准张量积。

<span id="S152"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S152

**Original:** One can view S(x) as an infinite-dimensional feature representation of the path x, with higher signature levels S^(m)(x) (the level-m term of S(x); this notation is used throughout) encoding increasingly high-order interactions between the d channels.

**中文:** 可将 S(x) 视为路径 x 的无限维特征表示，其中较高阶 signature 分量 S^(m)(x)（即 S(x) 的第 m 阶项；本文均采用此记号）编码 d 个通道之间逐渐升阶的交互作用。

<span id="S153"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S153

**Original:** Definition 14 (Time augmentation). The signature is invariant under continuous non-decreasing time reparameterizations [5, Lemma 1.2.1]. In many machine-learning settings this is undesirable, since we want time-series representations to also depend on their time parameterization. Following prior work [2,26], we use the standard time-augmentation trick. For x:[a,b]→V, define the augmented path x̄:[a,b]→R×V by x̄(t):=(t,x(t)). Throughout, apply the signature transform to x̄ rather than x; for notational simplicity, suppress the bar below. The signature is also invariant under constant translations of x (see Section 1.4 in Cass and Salvi [5]). We therefore either fix x(a)=0 or treat paths modulo translation.

**中文:** 定义 14（时间增强）。signature 对连续非递减的时间重参数化不变[5，引理 1.2.1]。在许多机器学习设置中，这种不变性并不理想，因为我们希望时间序列表示也依赖于其时间参数化方式。遵循已有工作[2,26]，本文采用标准的时间增强技巧。对于 x:[a,b]→V，定义增强路径 x̄:[a,b]→R×V 为 x̄(t):=(t,x(t))。下文均对 x̄ 而非 x 施加 signature 变换；为简化记号，后文省略上横线。signature 还对 x 的常数平移不变（见 Cass 和 Salvi[5]第 1.4 节）。因此，本文要么固定 x(a)=0，要么将路径视为模去平移等价类。

<span id="S154"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S154

**Original:** Remark 1 (Tree-like equivalence without time augmentation). Without the additional time coordinate, the signature is injective only up to tree-like equivalence [5, Section 1.4]. Accordingly, statements such as universality and characteristicness of the signature kernel on a set K should be understood as holding on the corresponding quotient space of equivalence classes when time augmentation is not used.

**中文:** 注 1（未进行时间增强时的树状等价）。若不添加时间坐标，signature 仅在树状等价意义下具有单射性[5，第 1.4 节]。因此，在未使用时间增强时，关于集合 K 上 signature 核的通用性和特征性的表述，应理解为在相应的等价类商空间上成立。

<span id="S155"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S155

**Original:** When working directly with signatures, one typically truncates S(x) at level L, defining S_{≤L}(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),...,S^(L)(x)). However, to compare paths x,y∈C_p and define kernel-induced discrepancies between distributions, the signature kernel can avoid truncation by working with inner products on the full tensor algebra. Concretely, equip V with an inner product ⟨·,·⟩_V. This induces canonical inner products ⟨·,·⟩_m on each tensor space V^{⊗m}, and hence an inner product ⟨·,·⟩_T on T by linearity across levels.

**中文:** 直接使用 signature 时，通常将 S(x) 截断到 L 阶，定义 S_{≤L}(x)=(1,S^(1)(x),...,S^(L)(x))。然而，为比较 x,y∈C_p 中的路径并定义分布间核诱导差异，可通过完整张量代数上的内积使用 signature 核，从而避免截断。具体而言，给 V 配备内积 ⟨·,·⟩_V，由此在每个张量空间 V^{⊗m} 上诱导规范内积 ⟨·,·⟩_m，并通过各阶上的线性结构在 T 上定义内积 ⟨·,·⟩_T。



## p.21 <span id="page-21"></span>

<span id="S156"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S156

**Original:** Definition 15 (Signature kernel). For paths x,y∈C_p, the signature kernel κ_sig:C_p×C_p→R is defined as the inner product of their signatures: κ_sig(x,y):=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩_T. It is symmetric and positive semidefinite and thus defines an RKHS on C_p. Crucially, κ_sig can be computed without explicitly forming signature tensors, via the signature kernel trick [42, Theorem 2.5]. In particular, κ_sig(x,y)=K(b,b), where K:[a,b]²→R is the signature kernel between two path prefixes, K(s,t):=⟨S(x_[a,s]),S(y_[a,t])⟩, satisfying K(s,t)=1+∫_a^s∫_a^t K(u,v)⟨dx_u,dy_v⟩_V. When x,y are continuously differentiable, this reduces to a linear hyperbolic Goursat-type PDE with boundary conditions K(a,·)=K(·,a)=1, for which standard finite-difference solvers apply. See Salvi et al. [42, Section 3.1] for one scheme. We use PYSIGLIB [44] for efficient computations.

**中文:** 定义 15（signature 核）。对于路径 x,y∈C_p，signature 核 κ_sig:C_p×C_p→R 定义为其 signature 的内积：κ_sig(x,y):=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩_T。该核对称且半正定，因此在 C_p 上定义一个 RKHS。关键在于，利用 Salvi 等人[42，定理 2.5]提出的 signature 核技巧，无需显式构造 signature 张量即可计算 κ_sig。具体而言，κ_sig(x,y)=K(b,b)，其中 K:[a,b]²→R 表示两条路径前缀之间的 signature 核，K(s,t):=⟨S(x_[a,s]),S(y_[a,t])⟩，并满足积分方程 K(s,t)=1+∫_a^s∫_a^t K(u,v)⟨dx_u,dy_v⟩_V。当 x、y 连续可微时，该积分方程化为带边界条件 K(a,·)=K(·,a)=1 的线性双曲型 Goursat 偏微分方程，可用标准有限差分求解器求解。一个有限差分方案见 Salvi 等人[42，第 3.1 节]。本文使用 PYSIGLIB[44]高效计算 signature 核。

<span id="S157"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S157

**Original:** Proposition 2 (Universality of the signature kernel [5]). Let C be a path space with a topology such that the signature transform S:C→T is continuous into a tensor Hilbert space T for which κ_sig(x,y):=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩_T is well-defined. Then, on compact K⊂C, κ_sig restricted to K×K is universal in the sense of Definition 10 and hence characteristic to P(K). A precise statement and proof of universality are in Cass and Salvi [5, Theorem 2.2.3]. Characteristicness on compact K follows from the relation between universality and characteristicness in [5, Theorem 2.2.7].

**中文:** 命题 2（signature 核的通用性[5]）。设 C 为路径空间，配备一种拓扑，使 signature 变换 S:C→T 连续映射到张量 Hilbert 空间 T，且 κ_sig(x,y):=⟨S(x),S(y)⟩_T 定义良好。则对任意紧集 K⊂C，κ_sig 在 K×K 上的限制具有定义 10 意义下的通用性，因而对 P(K) 具有特征性。通用性的精确表述和证明见 Cass 和 Salvi[5，定理 2.2.3]；紧集 K 上的特征性由[5，定理 2.2.7]中通用性与特征性的关系推出。

<span id="S158"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S158

**Original:** For a more detailed overview of signatures and signature kernels, see Cass and Salvi [5]. The compact-set universality and characteristicness statements used here are specifically in Chapter 2.2.

**中文:** signature 与 signature 核的详细综述见 Cass 和 Salvi[5]。本文使用的紧集通用性和特征性结论具体见该文第 2.2 章。

<span id="S159"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S159

**Original:** A.5 Optimal Transport and Sinkhorn Divergence

**中文:** A.5 最优传输与 Sinkhorn 散度

<span id="S160"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S160

**Original:** This section introduces the static optimal transport problem and its entropy-regularized version, connecting to the theory of statistical divergences. Let X,Y be metric spaces with Borel σ-algebras B(X),B(Y), let μ∈P(X) and ν∈P(Y) be probability measures, and let c:X×Y→R be a measurable cost. Definition 16 (Coupling). Let pr_X(x,y)=x and pr_Y(x,y)=y be the coordinate projections. The set of couplings (transport plans) is Π(μ,ν):={π∈P(X×Y):(pr_X)_#π=μ,(pr_Y)_#π=ν}.

**中文:** 本节介绍静态最优传输问题及其熵正则形式，并联系统计散度理论。设 X、Y 为度量空间，分别配备 Borel σ-代数 B(X)、B(Y)；令 μ∈P(X)、ν∈P(Y) 为概率测度，c:X×Y→R 为可测代价函数。定义 16（耦合）。令 pr_X(x,y)=x、pr_Y(x,y)=y 为坐标投影，则耦合集合（传输计划）为 Π(μ,ν):={π∈P(X×Y):(pr_X)_#π=μ,(pr_Y)_#π=ν}。

<span id="S161"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S161

**Original:** Definition 17 (Kantorovich Problem). The static optimal transport cost associated with c is OT_c(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)π(dx,dy). Any minimizer π*∈Π(μ,ν), if it exists, is an optimal transport plan.

**中文:** 定义 17（Kantorovich 问题）。与代价 c 对应的静态最优传输代价为 OT_c(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)π(dx,dy)。若极小化解 π*∈Π(μ,ν) 存在，则称其为最优传输计划。

<span id="S162"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S162

**Original:** Definition 18 (Wasserstein-p metrics). If X=Y is a metric space (X,d) and c(x,y)=d(x,y)^p with p≥1, define W_p(μ,ν):=[inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×X}d(x,y)^p dπ(x,y)]^{1/p}, for μ,ν∈P_p(X):={ρ∈P(X):∫d(x,x_0)^p dρ(x)<∞}.

**中文:** 定义 18（Wasserstein-p 度量）。若 X=Y 为度量空间 (X,d)，且 c(x,y)=d(x,y)^p，其中 p≥1，则定义 W_p(μ,ν):=[inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×X}d(x,y)^p dπ(x,y)]^{1/p}，其中 μ,ν∈P_p(X):={ρ∈P(X):∫d(x,x_0)^p dρ(x)<∞}。



## p.22 <span id="page-22"></span>

<span id="S163"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S163

**Original:** In the main text, W(μ,ν) denotes OT_c(μ,ν) with quadratic cost c=d², i.e. W_2(μ,ν). Next we present the Kantorovich dual form, clarifying ambient-space conditions for attainment of the primal infimum. Definition 19 (Polish space). A topological space (X,τ) is Polish if there is a metric d:X×X→[0,∞) such that: (1) d generates τ; (2) (X,d) is complete; (3) (X,d) is separable, with a countable dense subset D⊂X. Equivalently, a metric space is Polish iff complete and separable.

**中文:** 正文中，W(μ,ν) 表示采用二次代价 c=d² 的 OT_c(μ,ν)，即 W_2(μ,ν)。下面给出 Kantorovich 对偶形式，以说明原问题下确界能够达到所需的环境空间条件。定义 19（Polish 空间）。若拓扑空间 (X,τ) 上存在度量 d:X×X→[0,∞)，满足：（1）d 生成 τ；（2）(X,d) 完备；（3）(X,d) 可分，即存在可数稠密子集 D⊂X，则称 (X,τ) 为 Polish 空间。等价地，度量空间为 Polish 空间当且仅当其完备且可分。

<span id="S164"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S164

**Original:** Theorem 9 (Kantorovich duality on Polish spaces). Let (X,d_X),(Y,d_Y) be Polish spaces, μ∈P(X), ν∈P(Y). Assume c:X×Y→(−∞,+∞] is lower semicontinuous and bounded below, and at least one coupling π_0∈Π(μ,ν) satisfies ∫c dπ_0<∞. Then inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)dπ(x,y)=sup{∫_Xφdμ+∫_Yψdν:φ(x)+ψ(y)≤c(x,y) for all (x,y)}, where the supremum is over Borel φ:X→R∪{−∞}, ψ:Y→R∪{−∞} with φ∈L¹(μ), ψ∈L¹(ν). Moreover, the infimum is attained by an optimal plan π*∈Π(μ,ν).

**中文:** 定理 9（Polish 空间上的 Kantorovich 对偶）。设 (X,d_X)、(Y,d_Y) 为 Polish 空间，μ∈P(X)、ν∈P(Y)。假设 c:X×Y→(−∞,+∞] 下半连续且有下界，并且至少存在一个耦合 π_0∈Π(μ,ν) 满足 ∫c dπ_0<∞。则 inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)dπ(x,y)=sup{∫_Xφdμ+∫_Yψdν: 对所有 (x,y)，φ(x)+ψ(y)≤c(x,y)}；上确界取遍满足 φ∈L¹(μ)、ψ∈L¹(ν) 的 Borel 函数 φ:X→R∪{−∞} 和 ψ:Y→R∪{−∞}。此外，该下确界由某个最优计划 π*∈Π(μ,ν) 达到。

<span id="S165"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S165

**Original:** This duality theorem is in Villani [49, Chapter 5], and attainment of the infimum on Polish spaces is in [49, Theorem 4.1]. For Theorem 2, attainment holds on both experimental function spaces: a separable Hilbert space with its norm topology is Polish, as is path space with its product topology [5, Lemma 1.4.13].

**中文:** 该对偶定理见 Villani[49，第 5 章]；Polish 空间上最优值达到的结论见[49，定理 4.1]。对于定理 2，本文实验中的两类函数空间均满足最优值可达：配备范数拓扑的可分 Hilbert 空间是 Polish 空间，配备乘积拓扑的路径空间也是 Polish 空间[5，引理 1.4.13]。

<span id="S166"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S166

**Original:** The theory of entropy-regularized static OT is in Peyré and Cuturi [38, Chapter 4], and its connection to statistical divergences in Chapter 8. We state only definitions needed for the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence in the main text. Entropy-regularized OT adds to transport cost a multiple of the Kullback–Leibler (KL) divergence of the plan from the product of its marginals. Definition 20 (Kullback–Leibler divergence). For probability measures P,Q on a measurable space (X,F), KL(P||Q):=∫_X log(dP/dQ)dP if P≪Q, and +∞ otherwise. Equivalently, if P≪Q, KL(P||Q)=∫_X(dP/dQ)log(dP/dQ)dQ, where dP/dQ is the Radon–Nikodym derivative (Q-a.e.).

**中文:** 熵正则静态 OT 理论见 Peyré 和 Cuturi[38，第 4 章]，其与统计散度的联系见第 8 章。这里仅列出正文 Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度所需的定义。熵正则 OT 在传输代价上加上耦合计划相对于其边缘分布乘积测度的 Kullback–Leibler（KL）散度项。定义 20（Kullback–Leibler 散度）。对可测空间 (X,F) 上的概率测度 P、Q，若 P≪Q，则 KL(P||Q):=∫_X log(dP/dQ)dP；否则 KL(P||Q):=+∞。等价地，当 P≪Q 时，KL(P||Q)=∫_X(dP/dQ)log(dP/dQ)dQ，其中 dP/dQ 为 Radon–Nikodym 导数（Q 几乎处处意义下）。

<span id="S167"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S167

**Original:** Definition 21 (Entropic OT Functional). OT_{c,ε}(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}{∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)}. Definition 22 (Sinkhorn Divergence). S_{c,ε}(μ,ν):=OT_{c,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{c,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{c,ε}(ν,ν).

**中文:** 定义 21（熵正则 OT 泛函）。OT_{c,ε}(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}{∫_{X×Y}c(x,y)dπ(x,y)+ε KL(π||μ⊗ν)}。定义 22（Sinkhorn 散度）。S_{c,ε}(μ,ν):=OT_{c,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{c,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{c,ε}(ν,ν)。

<span id="S168"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S168

**Original:** This is the entropic OT functional used in the proof of Theorem 2; it is well-defined because our ambient spaces are Polish. The Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence in the main text and Li et al. [31] is obtained by applying the same self-cost debiasing after restricting the ambient space to an RKHS with its norm-induced topology.

**中文:** 这是定理 2 证明中使用的熵正则 OT 泛函；由于环境空间为 Polish 空间，该泛函定义良好。正文及 Li 等人[31]提出的 Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度，是将环境空间限制为配备范数诱导拓扑的 RKHS 后，再采用相同的自代价去偏方法得到的。

**Original:** B.1 Inherited Results from Li et al. (2021)

**中文:** B.1 继承自 Li 等人（2021）的结果

<span id="S171"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S171

**Original:** For transparency, we separate prior results from our original proofs. Proposition 3 (the equivalence behind Equation (4)) and the following two propositions are inherited from Li et al. [31]. We restate them as standard ingredients in our function-space analysis, but do not claim them as new contributions; their proofs are referenced directly to the original source. Proposition 3 (Li et al. [31]). The entropic OT functional underlying Definition 1 is equivalently formulated as Equation (4): if π* minimizes Equation (4), its pushforward (φ⊗φ)_#π* minimizes Equation (3); applying the same self-cost subtraction yields S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)=S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν). Proof: Theorem 1 in the supplement of Li et al. [31]. Proposition 4. Let μ_n,ν_n be empirical estimators in Equation (5), and ε,η>0. Then there exists N>0 such that for all n≥N, P(|S_ε(μ_n,ν_n)−S_ε(μ,ν)|≤εη)=1. Proof: Theorem 2 in the supplement of Li et al. [31]. Proposition 5. Let ||f||_H≤M and B_η={f∈H:||f||_H<η}. Let m be the number of basis functions spanning functions f in B_η. For M,η,δ,ε>0, P(|S_ε(μ_n,ν_n)−S_ε(μ,ν)|≤εη)≥1−δ whenever n≥(1/η²)·2M²(log(2/δ)+m log(24M/η)). Proof: Proposition 3 in the supplement of Li et al. [31].

**中文:** 为确保透明，本文将已有结果与原创证明区分开来。命题 3（式（4）背后的等价关系）及下列两个命题继承自 Li 等人[31]。本文重述这些函数空间分析中的标准工具，但不将其宣称为新贡献；证明直接引用原文。命题 3（Li 等人[31]）。定义 1 对应的熵正则 OT 泛函可等价地写成式（4）：若 π* 是式（4）的极小化耦合，则其推前 (φ⊗φ)_#π* 是式（3）的极小化耦合；在两侧作相同的自代价相减即可得 S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)=S_{ε,κ}(φ_#μ,φ_#ν)。证明见 Li 等人[31]补充材料定理 1。命题 4。设 μ_n、ν_n 为式（5）中的经验估计量，且 ε、η>0。则存在 N>0，使得对所有 n≥N，P(|S_ε(μ_n,ν_n)−S_ε(μ,ν)|≤εη)=1。证明见 Li 等人[31]补充材料定理 2。命题 5。设 ||f||_H≤M，B_η={f∈H:||f||_H<η}，m 为张成 B_η 内函数 f 的基函数数量。对于 M、η、δ、ε>0，只要 n≥(1/η²)·2M²(log(2/δ)+m log(24M/η))，就有 P(|S_ε(μ_n,ν_n)−S_ε(μ,ν)|≤εη)≥1−δ。证明见 Li 等人[31]补充材料命题 3。

<span id="S172"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S172

**Original:** B.2 Absolute Continuity Behind the CFM/FM Equivalence

**中文:** B.2 CFM/FM 等价性背后的绝对连续条件

<span id="S173"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S173

**Original:** The equivalence between conditional and unconditional flow-matching losses in Section 2.1 rests on an absolute-continuity assumption worth making explicit in infinite dimensions. Two FFM conditionals N(tf,σ_t²C_0) and N(t′f′,σ_{t′}²C_0) are typically mutually singular (Feldman–Hájek), so the relevant condition is not conditional-versus-conditional but conditional-versus-mixture at each fixed t: μ_t^f≪μ_t for ν-a.e. f. A sufficient condition, used implicitly by FFM [25], is that ν is supported in the Cameron–Martin space H(C_0)=Ran(C_0^{1/2}) of the prior, together with σ_t bounded below (σ_min>0, enforced by Algorithm 1). Then at fixed t all conditionals {N(tf,σ_t²C_0): f∈suppν} are mutually equivalent by Cameron–Martin, hence absolutely continuous with respect to the mixture μ_t, justifying exchange of marginal and conditional velocities at each t. When data are rougher than the prior (suppν⊄H(C_0)), equivalence degrades, so prior roughness should match the data; this motivates selecting a white-noise base measure on the Navier–Stokes family (Appendix C.5).

**中文:** 第 2.1 节条件流匹配损失与无条件流匹配损失的等价性依赖绝对连续假设；在无限维情形下，有必要将该假设明确写出。两个 FFM 条件测度 N(tf,σ_t²C_0) 和 N(t′f′,σ_{t′}²C_0) 通常互相奇异（Feldman–Hájek 定理），因此相关条件并非“条件测度与条件测度”之间的关系，而是在每个固定 t 下“条件测度与混合测度”之间的关系：对 ν 几乎处处的 f，要求 μ_t^f≪μ_t。FFM[25]隐含采用的一个充分条件是：ν 支持于先验的 Cameron–Martin 空间 H(C_0)=Ran(C_0^{1/2})，且 σ_t 有正下界（σ_min>0，算法 1 保证这一点）。于是对固定 t，集合 {N(tf,σ_t²C_0): f∈suppν} 中的条件测度依据 Cameron–Martin 公式彼此等价，因此都绝对连续于混合测度 μ_t，从而在每个 t 上可以交换边缘速度和条件速度。若数据比先验更粗糙（suppν⊄H(C_0)），等价关系会变差，因此先验的粗糙度应与数据相匹配；这也是 Navier–Stokes 系列选择白噪声基础测度的动机之一（附录 C.5）。

<span id="S174"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S174

**Original:** How kFFM inherits this structure. At the population level, write G_t(f_0,f_1):=t f_1+σ_t f_0. The kFFM path marginal is μ_t^π=(G_t)_#π, the FFM marginal is μ_t=(G_t)_#(μ_0⊗ν), and the kFFM conditional given f_1 is μ_t^{f_1,π}=(G_t(·,f_1))_#π(·|f_1), while the FFM conditional is N(tf_1,σ_t²C_0)=(G_t(·,f_1))_#μ_0. For the entropic problem with bounded cost (Theorem 1), the optimal plan has Gibbs form dπ/d(μ_0⊗ν)=exp{(φ⊕ψ−c_κ)/ε} with bounded potentials, so its density is bounded above and away from zero. Hence π∼μ_0⊗ν and π(·|f_1)∼μ_0.

**中文:** kFFM 如何继承这一结构。在总体层面，记 G_t(f_0,f_1):=t f_1+σ_t f_0。kFFM 的路径边缘测度为 μ_t^π=(G_t)_#π；FFM 的边缘测度为 μ_t=(G_t)_#(μ_0⊗ν)。给定 f_1 时，kFFM 的条件测度为 μ_t^{f_1,π}=(G_t(·,f_1))_#π(·|f_1)，而 FFM 的条件测度为 N(tf_1,σ_t²C_0)=(G_t(·,f_1))_#μ_0。对于有界代价下的熵正则问题（定理 1），最优计划具有 Gibbs 形式 dπ/d(μ_0⊗ν)=exp{(φ⊕ψ−c_κ)/ε}，其中势函数有界，因此其密度有有限上界并与零保持正距离。由此 π∼μ_0⊗ν，且 π(·|f_1)∼μ_0。



## p.24 <span id="page-24"></span>

<span id="S175"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S175

**Original:** For ν-a.e. f_1, μ_t^{f_1,π}∼N(tf_1,σ_t²C_0); since equivalence of measures is preserved under pushforward, μ_t^π∼μ_t, and every absolute-continuity relation used by FFM transfers to kFFM. Two remarks. First, this is where entropic regularization matters: an unregularized OT plan can be singular with respect to the product measure, invalidating the argument. Second, minibatch plans used in training are discrete surrogates of this population object (Section 3.1); the statement concerns the coupling they approximate.

**中文:** 对 ν 几乎处处的 f_1，μ_t^{f_1,π}∼N(tf_1,σ_t²C_0)。由于测度等价关系在推前映射下保持，因此 μ_t^π∼μ_t，FFM 所依赖的所有绝对连续关系也都可直接迁移到 kFFM。补充两点。第一，熵正则化在此处至关重要：未正则化的 OT 计划可能与乘积测度奇异，从而使上述论证失效。第二，训练中使用的小批次计划是总体对象的离散替代量（第 3.1 节）；本结论针对的是这些小批次计划所逼近的耦合。

<span id="S176"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S176

**Original:** B.3 Proof of Well-posedness and Uniform Boundedness (Theorem 1). Theorem 1: Let (X,||·||_X) be a real Banach space, d_X(f,g)=||f−g||_X, μ,ν∈P(X), and κ:X×X→R measurable, symmetric, positive definite with RKHS H_κ. Assume B_κ:=sup_{f∈X}κ(f,f)<∞. Then 0≤c_κ(f,g)≤4B_κ and for every ε>0, S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν) is finite with |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤8B_κ. Proof. Write S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)=OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν). By the reproducing property, ||φ(f)||²_{H_κ}=⟨κ(·,f),κ(·,f)⟩_{H_κ}=κ(f,f)≤B_κ. Thus c_κ(f,g)=||φ(f)−φ(g)||²_{H_κ}≤2||φ(f)||²+2||φ(g)||²≤4B_κ. Fix ε>0. Since c_κ≥0 and KL≥0, OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≥0. The feasible coupling π_0=μ⊗ν has KL(π_0||μ⊗ν)=0, hence OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤∫c_κd(μ⊗ν)≤4B_κ. Therefore 0≤OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤4B_κ<∞; the same applies to both self-costs. Each of the three terms in S lies in [0,4B_κ], so S is finite and |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)+(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)+(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν)≤4B_κ+2B_κ+2B_κ=8B_κ.

**中文:** B.3 良定性与一致有界性的证明（定理 1）。定理 1：设 (X,||·||_X) 为实 Banach 空间，d_X(f,g)=||f−g||_X，μ,ν∈P(X)，且 κ:X×X→R 为可测、对称、正定核，其 RKHS 为 H_κ。假设 B_κ:=sup_{f∈X}κ(f,f)<∞。则 0≤c_κ(f,g)≤4B_κ，且对任意 ε>0，S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν) 有限并满足 |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤8B_κ。证明。写作 S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)=OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)−(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν)。根据再生性质，||φ(f)||²_{H_κ}=⟨κ(·,f),κ(·,f)⟩_{H_κ}=κ(f,f)≤B_κ。因此 c_κ(f,g)=||φ(f)−φ(g)||²_{H_κ}≤2||φ(f)||²+2||φ(g)||²≤4B_κ。固定 ε>0。由于 c_κ≥0 且 KL≥0，故 OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≥0。可行耦合 π_0=μ⊗ν 满足 KL(π_0||μ⊗ν)=0，因此 OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤∫c_κd(μ⊗ν)≤4B_κ。于是 0≤OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤4B_κ<∞；两个自代价同理。S 中三个项均属于 [0,4B_κ]，故 S 有限，并且 |S_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)|≤OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)+(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(μ,μ)+(1/2)OT_{κ,ε}(ν,ν)≤4B_κ+2B_κ+2B_κ=8B_κ。

<span id="S177"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S177

**Original:** Interpretation. Theorem 1 provides the well-posedness step needed in function space: a bounded kernel gives a cost uniformly bounded by 4B_κ and a finite HSD objective for all probability measures on the ambient Banach space, with no moment assumptions. This uniform bound conditions the entropic solver (Section 3.2) and is the hypothesis left implicit in the inherited equivalence proposition from Li et al. [31].

**中文:** 解释。定理 1 给出了函数空间问题所需的良定性步骤：只要核有界，代价就一致有界于 4B_κ，且环境 Banach 空间上的任意概率测度对应的 HSD 目标均有限，不需要对测度施加矩条件。该一致界改善熵正则求解器的条件（第 3.2 节），也是 Li 等人[31]继承等价命题中未明确写出的假设。

<span id="S178"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S178

**Original:** B.4 Proof of the Error Decomposition (Theorem 2). Theorem 2 (Error decomposition). Let (X,d) be a compact metric space and μ,ν∈P(X). Define c(x,y)=d(x,y)². Let κ be a bounded positive-definite kernel with RKHS-induced cost c_κ(x,y)=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y), and Δ_κ=sup_{x,y∈X}|c_κ(x,y)−c(x,y)|. Let OT_{κ,ε} be the entropic OT functional in Equation (4) with cost c_κ, and M=diam(X)<∞. Then for every ε>0 and δ∈(0,M], |OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+2εlogN(X,δ;d)+8Mδ, (6) where N(X,δ;d) is the δ-covering number of X in metric d.

**中文:** B.4 误差分解的证明（定理 2）。定理 2（误差分解）。设 (X,d) 为紧度量空间，μ,ν∈P(X)，并定义 c(x,y)=d(x,y)²。设 κ 为有界正定核，其 RKHS 诱导代价 c_κ(x,y)=κ(x,x)+κ(y,y)−2κ(x,y)，且 Δ_κ=sup_{x,y∈X}|c_κ(x,y)−c(x,y)|。令 OT_{κ,ε} 为采用代价 c_κ 的式（4）熵正则 OT 泛函，并令 M=diam(X)<∞。则对任意 ε>0 及 δ∈(0,M]，|OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+2εlogN(X,δ;d)+8Mδ。（6）其中 N(X,δ;d) 是度量 d 下 X 的 δ-覆盖数。



## p.25 <span id="page-25"></span>

<span id="S179"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S179

**Original:** Proof of Theorem 2. Let c(x,y)=d(x,y)² and c_κ be the RKHS-induced cost. By definition of Δ_κ, for every coupling π∈Π(μ,ν), |∫c_κdπ−∫cdπ|≤Δ_κ. Therefore the unregularized OT values for the two costs differ by at most Δ_κ. For a lower bound, non-negativity of KL gives OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≥inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫c_κdπ≥W(μ,ν)−Δ_κ.

**中文:** 定理 2 的证明。令 c(x,y)=d(x,y)²，c_κ 为 RKHS 诱导代价。根据 Δ_κ 的定义，对每个耦合 π∈Π(μ,ν)，均有 |∫c_κdπ−∫cdπ|≤Δ_κ。因此，两种代价对应的非正则 OT 值相差至多 Δ_κ。为证明下界，利用 KL 散度非负，有 OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≥inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫c_κdπ≥W(μ,ν)−Δ_κ。

<span id="S180"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S180

**Original:** For the upper bound, let m=N(X,δ;d) and choose centers x_1,...,x_m whose closed δ-balls cover X. A measurable quantization map Q:X→{x_1,...,x_m} with d(x,Q(x))≤δ is obtained by assigning each point to the first center whose ball contains it, giving a finite Borel partition C_i=Q^{-1}({x_i}). Since X is compact and c=d² is continuous, an optimal coupling π* for W(μ,ν) exists. Set p_ij=π*(C_i×C_j), a_i=μ(C_i), b_j=ν(C_j). For cells of positive mass, let μ_i,ν_j be the conditional restrictions of μ,ν to C_i,C_j; zero-mass cells may be assigned arbitrary probability measures. Define π̂=Σ_{i,j}p_ij μ_i⊗ν_j. This preserves marginals, so π̂∈Π(μ,ν). On C_i×C_j with a_i b_j>0, its density relative to μ⊗ν is p_ij/(a_i b_j), and zero-weight cells have p_ij=0. Hence KL(π̂||μ⊗ν)=KL(p||a⊗b)≤2logm, since KL(p||a⊗b)=H(a)+H(b)−H(p)≤H(a)+H(b)≤2logm.

**中文:** 为证明上界，令 m=N(X,δ;d)，并选取中心 x_1,...,x_m，使其闭 δ 球覆盖 X。将每个点分配给包含它的第一个中心，可得到可测量化映射 Q:X→{x_1,...,x_m}，满足 d(x,Q(x))≤δ；由此得到有限 Borel 划分 C_i=Q^{-1}({x_i})。由于 X 紧且 c=d² 连续，W(μ,ν) 的最优耦合 π* 存在。令 p_ij=π*(C_i×C_j)，a_i=μ(C_i)，b_j=ν(C_j)。对正测度单元，令 μ_i、ν_j 为 μ、ν 在 C_i、C_j 上的条件限制；零测度单元可赋予任意概率测度。定义 π̂=Σ_{i,j}p_ij μ_i⊗ν_j。该构造保持边缘分布，因此 π̂∈Π(μ,ν)。在满足 a_i b_j>0 的 C_i×C_j 上，π̂ 相对于 μ⊗ν 的密度为 p_ij/(a_i b_j)；权重为零的单元满足 p_ij=0。因此 KL(π̂||μ⊗ν)=KL(p||a⊗b)≤2logm，因为 KL(p||a⊗b)=H(a)+H(b)−H(p)≤H(a)+H(b)≤2logm。

<span id="S181"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S181

**Original:** Moreover, for x∈C_i and y∈C_j, |d(x,y)²−d(x_i,x_j)²|≤(d(x,y)+d(x_i,x_j))|d(x,y)−d(x_i,x_j)|≤4Mδ. Applying this once under π̂ and once under π* gives ∫c dπ̂≤W(μ,ν)+8Mδ. Using again ∫c_κdπ̂≤∫c dπ̂+Δ_κ and feasibility of π̂ for the entropic problem yields OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤W(μ,ν)+Δ_κ+8Mδ+2εlogm. Combining lower and upper bounds and substituting m=N(X,δ;d) proves the result.

**中文:** 此外，对 x∈C_i、y∈C_j，有 |d(x,y)²−d(x_i,x_j)²|≤(d(x,y)+d(x_i,x_j))|d(x,y)−d(x_i,x_j)|≤4Mδ。分别在 π̂ 和 π* 下应用该界，得到 ∫c dπ̂≤W(μ,ν)+8Mδ。再次利用 ∫c_κdπ̂≤∫c dπ̂+Δ_κ，并注意 π̂ 是熵正则问题的可行解，可得 OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)≤W(μ,ν)+Δ_κ+8Mδ+2εlogm。合并上下界，并代入 m=N(X,δ;d)，即得结论。

<span id="S182"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S182

**Original:** Euclidean covering-rate special case. If X⊂R^d has bounded radius M, its covering number satisfies logN(X,δ)≤C_d log(M/δ) for a universal constant C_d>0. Substituting into Theorem 2 gives |OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+C_d εd log(M/δ)+Mδ, after absorbing constants. When c_κ=c, this recovers the usual entropic-regularization rate up to constants.

**中文:** 欧几里得覆盖速率的特例。若 X⊂R^d 且半径有界 M，则其覆盖数满足 logN(X,δ)≤C_d log(M/δ)，其中 C_d>0 为仅依赖维数的常数。代入定理 2 并吸收常数后，得到 |OT_{κ,ε}(μ,ν)−W(μ,ν)|≤Δ_κ+C_d εd log(M/δ)+Mδ。当 c_κ=c 时，该式在常数因子意义下恢复通常的熵正则化速率。

<span id="S183"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S183

**Original:** B.5 Proof of Discretization Invariance (Theorem 3). Theorem 3 (Discretization invariance). Let Ω⊂R^d be a bounded periodic domain and α>k≥0. Set X:=B_R^{H^α}:={f∈H^α(Ω):||f||_{H^α}≤R}, viewed as a compact subset of H^k(Ω), and μ,ν∈P(X). For Fourier basis {e_n}_{n∈Z^d}, let V_N=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}} and P_N:H^α(Ω)→V_N be the orthogonal projection, hence dimV_N≍N. Write μ_N=(P_N)_#μ, ν_N=(P_N)_#ν. Define κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=exp(−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²)) on H^k and KOT_ε^(k)=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε}. Then for every ε>0, |KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. Equivalently, with grid spacing h=N^{−1/d}, the discretization error decays as O(h^{2(α−k)}).

**中文:** B.5 离散化不变性的证明（定理 3）。定理 3（离散化不变性）。设 Ω⊂R^d 为有界周期区域，且 α>k≥0。令 X:=B_R^{H^α}:={f∈H^α(Ω):||f||_{H^α}≤R}，将其视为 H^k(Ω) 的紧子集，并令 μ,ν∈P(X)。对于傅里叶基 {e_n}_{n∈Z^d}，令 V_N=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}}，P_N:H^α(Ω)→V_N 为正交投影，因此 dimV_N≍N。记 μ_N=(P_N)_#μ、ν_N=(P_N)_#ν。在 H^k 上定义 κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=exp(−||f−g||²_{H^k}/(2σ²))，并令 KOT_ε^(k)=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε}。则对任意 ε>0，有 |KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。等价地，若网格间距 h=N^{−1/d}，离散化误差按 O(h^{2(α−k)}) 衰减。



## p.26 <span id="page-26"></span>

<span id="S184"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S184

**Original:** Notation. Ω⊂R^d is the bounded periodic domain, {e_n}_{n∈Z^d} its Fourier basis, and ||f||²_{H^s}=Σ_{n∈Z^d}(1+|n|²)^s|f̂_n|², s≥0. P_N is the orthogonal projection onto V_N=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}}, so dimV_N≍N and the highest retained frequency is ≍N^{1/d}. Write KOT_ε^(k)=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε} and c_RBF^(k)(f,g)=||φ(f)−φ(g)||²_{H_κ}=2(1−κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)). Since B_R^{H^α} is compact in H^k by compact Sobolev embedding on bounded periodic domains [12], and c_RBF^(k) is bounded and continuous, the entropic kernel-OT functionals below attain minimizers.

**中文:** 记号。Ω⊂R^d 为有界周期区域，{e_n}_{n∈Z^d} 为其傅里叶基，Sobolev 范数为 ||f||²_{H^s}=Σ_{n∈Z^d}(1+|n|²)^s|f̂_n|²，s≥0。P_N 是到 V_N=span{e_n:|n|²≤N^{2/d}} 的正交投影，因此 dimV_N≍N，最高保留频率为 ≍N^{1/d}。记 KOT_ε^(k)=OT_{κ_RBF^(k),ε}，并令 c_RBF^(k)(f,g)=||φ(f)−φ(g)||²_{H_κ}=2(1−κ_RBF^(k)(f,g))。由于有界周期区域上的紧 Sobolev 嵌入[12]使 B_R^{H^α} 在 H^k 中紧，且 c_RBF^(k) 有界连续，以下熵正则核 OT 泛函均能达到其极小值。

<span id="S185"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S185

**Original:** Step 1: Sobolev approximation on the truncation tail. For any f∈H^α(Ω) with ||f||_{H^α}≤R and 0≤k<α, ||f−P_Nf||²_{H^k}=Σ_{|n|²>N^{2/d}}(1+|n|²)^k|f̂_n|²=Σ_{|n|²>N^{2/d}}(1+|n|²)^{−(α−k)}(1+|n|²)^α|f̂_n|²≤(1+N^{2/d})^{−(α−k)}||f||²_{H^α}≤R²N^{−2(α−k)/d}. Hence ||f−P_Nf||_{H^k}≤RN^{−(α−k)/d} for all f∈B_R^{H^α}. (7)

**中文:** 步骤 1：截断尾部的 Sobolev 逼近。对任意满足 ||f||_{H^α}≤R、0≤k<α 的 f∈H^α(Ω)，有 ||f−P_Nf||²_{H^k}=Σ_{|n|²>N^{2/d}}(1+|n|²)^k|f̂_n|²=Σ_{|n|²>N^{2/d}}(1+|n|²)^{−(α−k)}(1+|n|²)^α|f̂_n|²≤(1+N^{2/d})^{−(α−k)}||f||²_{H^α}≤R²N^{−2(α−k)/d}。因此，对所有 f∈B_R^{H^α}，有 ||f−P_Nf||_{H^k}≤RN^{−(α−k)/d}。（7）

<span id="S186"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S186

**Original:** Step 2: Stability of the squared H^k distance under simultaneous projection. For f,g∈B_R^{H^α}, since P_N is an orthogonal projection on H^k, ||f−g||²_{H^k}−||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k}=||(I−P_N)(f−g)||²_{H^k}≤||f−g||²_{H^α}N^{−2(α−k)/d}≤4R²N^{−2(α−k)/d}, using ||f−g||_{H^α}≤2R and Step 1 applied to f−g.

**中文:** 步骤 2：同时投影时 H^k 平方距离的稳定性。对 f,g∈B_R^{H^α}，由于 P_N 是 H^k 上的正交投影，||f−g||²_{H^k}−||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k}=||(I−P_N)(f−g)||²_{H^k}≤||f−g||²_{H^α}N^{−2(α−k)/d}≤4R²N^{−2(α−k)/d}；这里使用了 ||f−g||_{H^α}≤2R，以及对 f−g 应用步骤 1。

<span id="S187"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S187

**Original:** Step 3: Cost stability for the RBF kernel. Let h(s)=e^{−s/(2σ²)} for s≥0, so κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=h(||f−g||²_{H^k}) and |h′(s)|≤1/(2σ²). Then |c_RBF^(k)(f,g)−c_RBF^(k)(P_Nf,P_Ng)|=2|h(||f−g||²_{H^k})−h(||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k})|≤(1/σ²)| ||f−g||²_{H^k}−||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k} |≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. The bound is uniform over (f,g)∈B_R^{H^α}×B_R^{H^α}.

**中文:** 步骤 3：RBF 核代价的稳定性。令 h(s)=e^{−s/(2σ²)}，s≥0，则 κ_RBF^(k)(f,g)=h(||f−g||²_{H^k})，且 |h′(s)|≤1/(2σ²)。于是 |c_RBF^(k)(f,g)−c_RBF^(k)(P_Nf,P_Ng)|=2|h(||f−g||²_{H^k})−h(||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k})|≤(1/σ²)| ||f−g||²_{H^k}−||P_N(f−g)||²_{H^k} |≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。该界对 (f,g)∈B_R^{H^α}×B_R^{H^α} 一致成立。

<span id="S188"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S188

**Original:** Step 4: Upper bound. Let π*∈Π(μ,ν) be feasible for the continuum problem and define π̃=(P_N⊗P_N)_#π*∈Π(μ_N,ν_N). By Step 3, ∫_{V_N×V_N}c_RBF^(k)dπ̃=∫c_RBF^(k)(P_Nf,P_Ng)dπ*(f,g)≤∫c_RBF^(k)dπ*+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. By data-processing for KL under P_N⊗P_N, KL(π̃||μ_N⊗ν_N)≤KL(π*||μ⊗ν). Taking π* to optimize KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν), and using π̃ as a feasible point for KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N), gives KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)≤KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. (8)

**中文:** 步骤 4：上界。设 π*∈Π(μ,ν) 是连续空间问题的可行耦合，并定义 π̃=(P_N⊗P_N)_#π*∈Π(μ_N,ν_N)。根据步骤 3，∫_{V_N×V_N}c_RBF^(k)dπ̃=∫c_RBF^(k)(P_Nf,P_Ng)dπ*(f,g)≤∫c_RBF^(k)dπ*+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。根据 P_N⊗P_N 映射下 KL 散度的数据处理不等式，KL(π̃||μ_N⊗ν_N)≤KL(π*||μ⊗ν)。取 π* 为 KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν) 的最优耦合，并以 π̃ 作为 KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N) 的可行解，可得 KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)≤KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。（8）


## p.27 <span id="page-27"></span>

<span id="S189"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S189

**Original:** Step 5: Lower bound via gluing. Let π⋆_N∈Π(μ_N,ν_N) be an optimizer for KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N). Disintegrate μ along P_N: since compact metric spaces are standard Borel, the standard regular conditional probability theorem applies [10]. Thus there exist Borel kernels μ(·|u), u∈V_N, such that μ(A)=∫_{V_N} μ(A|u)dμ_N(u), A⊂X Borel, with μ(·|u) supported on the fibre P_N^{-1}(u) for μ_N-a.e. u, and similarly for ν(·|v). Define the lifted coupling π̂(A×B):=∫_{V_N×V_N} μ(A|u)ν(B|v)dπ⋆_N(u,v), A,B⊂X Borel. Direct verification gives π̂∈Π(μ,ν) and (P_N⊗P_N)_#π̂=π⋆_N. Since μ_N⊗ν_N is feasible with finite KL, the optimizer π⋆_N has finite KL and hence π⋆_N≪μ_N⊗ν_N. On each fibre product, the Radon–Nikodym derivative of π̂ with respect to μ⊗ν is exactly the derivative of π⋆_N with respect to μ_N⊗ν_N evaluated at (u,v)=(P_Nf,P_Ng). Therefore, by the chain rule for KL divergence, KL(π̂∥μ⊗ν)=KL(π⋆_N∥μ_N⊗ν_N).

**中文:** 步骤 5：通过粘合得到下界。设 π⋆_N∈Π(μ_N,ν_N) 是 KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N) 的最优解。沿 P_N 对 μ 作析取：由于紧致度量空间是标准 Borel 空间，标准正则条件概率定理适用[10]。因此存在 Borel 核 μ(·|u)，u∈V_N，使得对 Borel 集 A⊂X，μ(A)=∫_{V_N}μ(A|u)dμ_N(u)；并且对 μ_N-几乎处处的 u，μ(·|u) 支撑在纤维 P_N^{-1}(u) 上。ν(·|v) 也有相同的析取表示。定义提升后的耦合 π̂(A×B):=∫_{V_N×V_N}μ(A|u)ν(B|v)dπ⋆_N(u,v)，其中 A、B⊂X 为 Borel 集。直接验证可得 π̂∈Π(μ,ν)，且 (P_N⊗P_N)_#π̂=π⋆_N。由于 μ_N⊗ν_N 是具有有限 KL 散度的可行耦合，最优解 π⋆_N 的 KL 散度有限，因此 π⋆_N≪μ_N⊗ν_N。在每个纤维积上，π̂ 相对于 μ⊗ν 的 Radon–Nikodym 导数，恰为 π⋆_N 相对于 μ_N⊗ν_N 的导数在 (u,v)=(P_Nf,P_Ng) 处的取值。因此，根据 KL 散度的链式法则，KL(π̂∥μ⊗ν)=KL(π⋆_N∥μ_N⊗ν_N)。

<span id="S190"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S190

**Original:** For the cost integral, Step 3 applied in reverse gives ∫ c_RBF^(k)dπ̂ ≤ ∫_{V_N×V_N}c_RBF^(k)dπ⋆_N +(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}, using that (f,g)∼π̂ projects to (u,v)∼π⋆_N and that the cost difference is bounded uniformly. Hence KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)≤KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. (9)

**中文:** 对于代价积分，反向应用步骤 3 可得 ∫c_RBF^(k)dπ̂≤∫_{V_N×V_N}c_RBF^(k)dπ⋆_N+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。这是因为 (f,g)∼π̂ 投影后得到 (u,v)∼π⋆_N，且代价差一致有界。因此 KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)≤KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。（9）

<span id="S191"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S191

**Original:** Step 6: Conclusion. Combining (8) and (9) yields |KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}.

**中文:** 步骤 6：结论。合并式（8）和式（9）可得 |KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)|≤(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。

<span id="S192"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S192

**Original:** B.6 Composed End-to-End Bound. Corollary 1 (Composed end-to-end bound). Let X=B_R^{H^α} be as in Theorem 3, equipped with d_k(f,g):=∥f−g∥_{H^k}, and let W_k(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×X}∥f−g∥²_{H^k}dπ(f,g) denote quadratic-cost OT in the H^k metric. Write M_k:=diam(X;d_k) and let N_k(δ):=N(X,δ;d_k) be the corresponding covering number. For the Sobolev-RBF kernel in Theorem 3, define the mismatch Δ_RBF:=Δ_{κ_RBF^(k)}, i.e., Δ_RBF:=sup_{f,g∈X}|2(1−e^{−∥f−g∥²_{H^k}/(2σ²)})−∥f−g∥²_{H^k}|≤M_k²+2, where the bound follows because the RBF-induced cost lies in [0,2] and ∥f−g∥²_{H^k}∈[0,M_k²] on X. Then for every ε>0, δ∈(0,M_k] and every projection rank N∈N, |KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−W_k(μ,ν)|≤Δ_RBF+2ε log N_k(δ)+8M_kδ+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}. Proof. By the triangle inequality, |KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−W_k(μ,ν)|≤|KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)|+|KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−W_k(μ,ν)|. The first term is bounded by Theorem 3; the second by Theorem 2 applied on the compact metric space (X,d_k) with the Sobolev-RBF kernel, whose mismatch is Δ_RBF.

**中文:** B.6 组合端到端误差界。推论 1（组合端到端误差界）。令 X=B_R^{H^α}，其定义同定理 3，并赋予度量 d_k(f,g):=∥f−g∥_{H^k}。令 W_k(μ,ν):=inf_{π∈Π(μ,ν)}∫_{X×X}∥f−g∥²_{H^k}dπ(f,g)，表示 H^k 度量下的二次代价最优传输（OT）。记 M_k:=diam(X;d_k)，并令 N_k(δ):=N(X,δ;d_k) 为相应的覆盖数。对于定理 3 中的 Sobolev-RBF 核，定义失配量 Δ_RBF:=Δ_{κ_RBF^(k)}，即 Δ_RBF:=sup_{f,g∈X}|2(1−e^{−∥f−g∥²_{H^k}/(2σ²)})−∥f−g∥²_{H^k}|≤M_k²+2。该界来自：RBF 诱导的代价属于 [0,2]，而在 X 上 ∥f−g∥²_{H^k}∈[0,M_k²]。于是，对任意 ε>0、δ∈(0,M_k] 及任意投影秩 N∈N，有 |KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−W_k(μ,ν)|≤Δ_RBF+2ε log N_k(δ)+8M_kδ+(4R²/σ²)N^{−2(α−k)/d}。证明：由三角不等式，|KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−W_k(μ,ν)|≤|KOT_ε^(k)(μ_N,ν_N)−KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)|+|KOT_ε^(k)(μ,ν)−W_k(μ,ν)|。第一项由定理 3 控制；第二项则由定理 2 在紧致度量空间 (X,d_k) 上针对 Sobolev-RBF 核应用得到，其代价失配量为 Δ_RBF。


## p.28 <span id="page-28"></span>

<span id="S193"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S193

**Original:** Remark. Of the four terms, only the discretization term vanishes with projection rank N (rate N^{−2(α−k)/d}, driven by the regularity gap α−k), the formal counterpart of the empirical resolution-invariance in Section 4. The corollary relies on compact Sobolev embedding [12], and our PDE benchmarks are used in the corresponding smooth-solution regime; such Sobolev and analytic regularity is standard in the PDE settings considered here, including Navier–Stokes in appropriate regimes [4]. The kernel-cost mismatch Δ_RBF, by contrast, depends only on kernel design: zero for the linear kernel, finite for RBF (controlled by data scale and bandwidth σ).

**中文:** 评注。四项中，只有离散化项会随投影秩 N 增大而趋于零，其速率为 N^{−2(α−k)/d}，由正则性差距 α−k 决定；这在形式上对应于第 4 节中的经验分辨率不变性。该推论依赖紧致 Sobolev 嵌入[12]。本文 PDE 基准所处的是相应的光滑解区域；对于本文考虑的 PDE 场景（包括适当条件下的 Navier–Stokes 方程），这类 Sobolev 正则性和解析正则性是标准性质[4]。相比之下，核代价失配量 Δ_RBF 仅取决于核的设计：线性核下为零，RBF 核下有限（由数据尺度和带宽 σ 控制）。

<span id="S194"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S194

**Original:** Remark (path space). For data on path space C_p([0,T],R^d) with p∈[1,2), the analogue of spectral truncation is signature truncation at level L, S_{≤L}(x):=(1,S^(1)(x),…,S^(L)(x)). Known factorial-decay bounds for signatures of finite-p-variation paths [5, Proposition 1.4.6] imply that, on compact sets with uniformly bounded p-variation control, the tail Σ_{ℓ>L}||S^(ℓ)(x)||² decays faster than any polynomial in L. By Cauchy–Schwarz, the full and truncated signature kernels are then uniformly close on such compact sets. Consequently, the corresponding entropic kernel-OT objectives differ by at most the uniform signature-kernel cost error, since the KL term is unchanged. This gives the same qualitative discretization-invariance message for path space, but with a factorial tail in the signature truncation level rather than a Sobolev Fourier-tail rate.

**中文:** 评注（路径空间）。对于路径空间 C_p([0,T],R^d) 上的数据（p∈[1,2)），谱截断的对应方法是将签名截断至 L 阶：S_{≤L}(x):=(1,S^(1)(x),…,S^(L)(x))。有限 p-变差路径的签名具有已知的阶乘衰减界[5，命题 1.4.6]，这意味着在 p-变差控制一致有界的紧集上，尾项 Σ_{ℓ>L}||S^(ℓ)(x)||² 随 L 的衰减快于任意多项式。由 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式可知，在这样的紧集上，完整签名核与截断签名核一致接近。因此，相应的熵正则核最优传输目标至多相差一致有界的签名核代价误差，因为 KL 项保持不变。这为路径空间提供了同样定性的离散化不变性结论，但其签名截断阶数对应的是阶乘尾项，而非 Sobolev Fourier 尾项速率。

**Source:** p.28 S195

**中文:** C 补充实验细节

<span id="S197"></span>

**Original:** C.1 Model Parameterization

**中文:** C.1 模型参数化

<span id="S198"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S198

**Original:** We implement Functional Flow Matching with the official implementation provided by its authors [footnote 3], without modifying any provided hyperparameter of the neural architecture, including σ_min=10^{−4}. The baseline models use the following hyperparameters, also following Kerrigan et al. [25]: (1) DDPM: noise schedule β_0=10^{−4}, β_T=0.02, and T=1000. (2) DDO (NCSN): time interval T=10; the noise schedule interpolates geometrically between σ_10=10^{−3} and σ_1=1 on 1D datasets, and between σ_10=10^{−2} and σ_1=100 on 2D datasets. (3) GANO: the generator is trained every 5 epochs, and gradient penalty λ=0.1 for 1D and λ=10 for 2D. Training. Baselines are trained with Adam, learning rate 10^{−3} on 1D and 5×10^{−4} on 2D data, except that the rate is 10^{−4} for GANO. This configuration also follows Kerrigan et al. [25]; no hyperparameter search was performed on the optimizer.

**中文:** 我们使用作者提供的官方实现[脚注 3]实现 Functional Flow Matching，未修改神经网络架构的任何给定超参数，包括 σ_min=10^{−4}。基线模型的超参数如下，同样遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]的设置：(1) DDPM：噪声调度为 β_0=10^{−4}、β_T=0.02，T=1000。(2) DDO（NCSN）：时间区间设为 T=10；在一维数据集上，噪声调度在 σ_10=10^{−3} 与 σ_1=1 之间作几何插值；在二维数据集上，则在 σ_10=10^{−2} 与 σ_1=100 之间作几何插值。(3) GANO：生成器每 5 个 epoch 训练一次；一维数据的梯度惩罚系数 λ=0.1，二维数据为 λ=10。训练设置：基线使用 Adam 优化器；一维数据的学习率为 10^{−3}，二维数据为 5×10^{−4}，GANO 的学习率例外设为 10^{−4}。此配置同样遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]；作者没有对优化器进行超参数搜索。

<span id="S199"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S199

**Original:** C.2 Baseline Description

**中文:** C.2 基线方法说明

<span id="S200"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S200

**Original:** We describe the baseline generative models used for the empirical comparisons. All operate in function space, and the roster covers the diffusion, flow-matching, adversarial, and score-based paradigms; every baseline uses the same neural-operator backbone and data pipeline as kFFM. 1. DDPM [24]: The DDPM used as a baseline is the functional DDPM model proposed by Kerrigan et al. [24], which generalizes the diffusion model in R^d using Gaussian measure theory in Hilbert space. Its formulation is analogous to the base DDPM but performs diffusion directly on Gaussian measures. 2. FFM [25]: Functional Flow Matching is the base model that we extend. It generalizes flow matching in R^d to infinite dimensions by formulating a path of Gaussian measures in a Hilbert space, in analogy with Kerrigan et al. [24]. In the terminology of Tong et al. [48], it couples prior and data samples independently, which leaves room for OT-based coupling.

**中文:** 本文说明用于实证比较的生成模型基线。它们均在函数空间中运行，涵盖扩散、流匹配、对抗式和基于分数的范式；所有基线都使用与 kFFM 相同的神经算子骨干网络和数据处理流程。1. DDPM[24]：作为基线的 DDPM 是 Kerrigan 等人[24]提出的函数型 DDPM 模型，它利用 Hilbert 空间中的高斯测度理论，将 R^d 中的扩散模型推广到函数空间。其形式与基础 DDPM 类似，但直接在高斯测度上执行扩散。2. FFM[25]：Functional Flow Matching 是本文扩展的基础模型。它通过在 Hilbert 空间中构造一条高斯测度路径，将 R^d 中的流匹配推广到无限维空间，其思路与 Kerrigan 等人[24]类似。按照 Tong 等人[48]的术语，该方法将先验样本与数据样本独立耦合，因此仍有采用基于 OT 的耦合方式的空间。


## p.29 <span id="page-29"></span>

<span id="S201"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S201

**Original:** 3. CFM-OT(L2) [48]: This is the direct finite-dimensional OT baseline used in the main text. It uses the same minibatch Sinkhorn solver and FNO backbone as our method, but applies OT to discretized vectors with the raw L2 cost and the white-noise (i.i.d. Gaussian) base measure, rather than a function-space kernel cost. 4. GANO [40]: The Generative Adversarial Neural Operator (GANO) is a generative adversarial network in function space whose generator maps Gaussian random fields to samples. 5. DDO/NCSN [34]: The Denoising Diffusion Operator (DDO) is the analogue of score-based generative models [45] in infinite dimensions. We follow Kerrigan et al. [25] and use the NCSN noise scale; the loss and noise schedule, including preconditioning, follow the code released with Li et al. [34].

**中文:** 3. CFM-OT(L2)[48]：这是正文中采用的直接有限维 OT 基线。它使用与本文方法相同的小批量 Sinkhorn 求解器和 FNO 骨干网络，但对离散向量应用原始 L2 代价和白噪声（独立同分布高斯）基准测度，而非函数空间核代价。4. GANO[40]：生成对抗神经算子（Generative Adversarial Neural Operator，GANO）是一种函数空间中的生成对抗网络，其生成器将高斯随机场映射为样本。5. DDO/NCSN[34]：去噪扩散算子（Denoising Diffusion Operator，DDO）是无限维空间中基于分数生成模型[45]的对应形式。本文遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]，采用 NCSN 噪声尺度；损失函数和噪声调度（包括预条件）遵循 Li 等人[34]发布的代码。

<span id="S202"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S202

**Original:** C.3 Dataset Description

**中文:** C.3 数据集说明

<span id="S203"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S203

**Original:** Of all the datasets we experiment on, AEMET, Gene expression, Economics are from Kerrigan et al. [25], Navier–Stokes, KdV, stochastic Navier–Stokes, stochastic KdV are from Salvi et al. [43], and Heston is from Issa et al. [23]. The descriptions below follow those sources; the architectures and training budgets of our runs are given in Appendix C.4.

**中文:** 本文实验数据集中，AEMET、基因表达和经济数据来自 Kerrigan 等人[25]；Navier–Stokes、KdV、随机 Navier–Stokes 和随机 KdV 数据来自 Salvi 等人[43]；Heston 数据来自 Issa 等人[23]。下述说明遵循这些来源；本文实验所用架构和训练预算见附录 C.4。

<span id="S204"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S204

**Original:** AEMET Dataset: This dataset comprises functional observations representing the mean annual profile of average daily temperature (°C) over 1980–2009, collected from 73 weather stations in Spain. Each curve is sampled on a uniform grid of length 365.

**中文:** AEMET 数据集：该数据集包含函数型观测，表示西班牙 73 个气象站在 1980–2009 年期间的日平均气温（°C）年均轮廓。每条曲线均在长度为 365 的均匀网格上采样。

<span id="S205"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S205

**Original:** Gene Expression: The full dataset contains 10,928 time series sampled at 20 evenly spaced time points, measuring gene-expression amplitudes for four genes. To produce a periodic spiking appearance while preserving the underlying structure, we concatenate the gene-specific sequences in time. Before training, the data are log-transformed and mean-centered. We then focus on 156 high-variability trajectories, selected by requiring the time-averaged standard deviation of each centered series to exceed 0.3.

**中文:** 基因表达数据：完整数据集包含 10,928 条时间序列，在 20 个等间隔时间点采样，测量四种基因的表达幅度。为在保留底层结构的同时形成周期性尖峰外观，作者按时间顺序拼接各基因对应的序列。训练前对数据取对数并进行均值中心化。随后聚焦于 156 条高变异轨迹，其筛选条件为：每条中心化序列的时间平均标准差大于 0.3。

<span id="S206"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S206

**Original:** Economic Dataset: We use the three economic datasets (population, GDP, labor) of Kerrigan et al. [25]; a separate model is trained on each, and the reported Economy metrics are averaged over the component series within each seed (the matched-seed comparisons of Tables 1 and 2 use the population and GDP series). As an example, the population dataset consists of time series tracking population changes for 169 countries worldwide from 1950 to 2018, sampled at 69 discrete time points. For visualization, each country’s series is normalized by its own mean, so the curves show population relative to that country’s average over the 69-year period. Overall, the trajectories display approximately linear growth with a common change point shared across countries.

**中文:** 经济数据集：本文使用 Kerrigan 等人[25]的三个经济数据集（人口、GDP、劳动力）；每个数据集分别训练一个模型。报告中的 Economy 指标是在每个随机种子内对各组成序列取平均得到的（表 1 和表 2 中配对种子比较使用人口和 GDP 序列）。例如，人口数据集由全球 169 个国家 1950–2018 年的人口变化时间序列构成，在 69 个离散时间点采样。为便于可视化，每个国家的序列均以自身均值归一化，因此曲线表示该国人口相对于 69 年期间平均人口的变化。总体上，各轨迹呈近似线性增长，并在各国间共享一个变化点。

<span id="S207"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S207

**Original:** Heston Dataset: The Heston model [21] is a stochastic volatility model with diffusion-driven, non-smooth sample paths. It models the asset price S_t and its variance ν_t as (in the standard Heston notation, which is local to this paragraph): dS_t=μS_tdt+√ν_t S_t dW^S_t; dν_t=κ(θ−ν_t)dt+ξ√ν_t dW^ν_t, where W^S_t,W^ν_t are Wiener processes with correlations. The Heston dataset is generated using the simulation code provided in the official repository of sigker-nsde [23], footnote 4. The script generates 5000 univariate time series of length 100 on [0,1]; we model the normalized log-variance paths. A long-horizon variant with 1000 timesteps (Heston-Long) is used only in the sensitivity study (“long volatility paths” of Appendices C.8 and C.16) and in the convergence diagnostic of Figure 6.

**中文:** Heston 数据集：Heston 模型[21]是一种随机波动率模型，其样本路径由扩散过程驱动且不光滑。模型描述资产价格 S_t 和方差 ν_t（以下采用仅限本段的标准 Heston 记号）：dS_t=μS_tdt+√ν_t S_t dW^S_t；dν_t=κ(θ−ν_t)dt+ξ√ν_t dW^ν_t，其中 W^S_t、W^ν_t 是具有相关性的 Wiener 过程。Heston 数据集由 sigker-nsde[23]官方代码库中的模拟代码生成[脚注 4]。脚本生成 5000 条定义在 [0,1] 上、长度为 100 的单变量时间序列；本文建模的是归一化对数方差路径。长时域版本包含 1000 个时间步（Heston-Long），仅用于敏感性研究（附录 C.8 和 C.16 中的“长波动率路径”）以及图 6 的收敛性诊断。

<span id="S208"></span>
**Source:** p.29 S208

**Original:** KdV and Stochastic KdV Equation: The stochastic Korteweg–de Vries equation is a higher-order SPDE given by ∂_t u+γ∂_x^3u=6u∂_x u+ξ, u(t,0)=u(t,1), u(0,x)=u_0(x), (t,x)∈[0,T]×[0,1].

**中文:** KdV 与随机 KdV 方程：随机 Korteweg–de Vries 方程是一种高阶随机偏微分方程（SPDE），其形式为 ∂_t u+γ∂_x^3u=6u∂_x u+ξ，边界条件为 u(t,0)=u(t,1)，初值条件为 u(0,x)=u_0(x)，且 (t,x)∈[0,T]×[0,1]。


## p.30 <span id="page-30"></span>

<span id="S209"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S209

**Original:** The base KdV equation simply removes the Q-Wiener process ξ. The KdV dataset is a single trajectory on a grid of 512 spatial points with 201 timesteps, and each time slice is one sample; the stochastic KdV dataset consists of 1200 trajectories on a grid of 128 spatial points with 101 timesteps each, again treated as snapshots. The simulation code for these equations is taken from the torch-spde repository [43], footnote 5. Navier–Stokes and Stochastic Navier–Stokes Equation: The 2D stochastic Navier–Stokes equation for an incompressible flow has the form ∂_t w−νΔw=−u·∇w+f+σξ, w(0,x)=w_0(x), (t,x)∈[0,T]×[0,1]^2, where the deterministic force is a function of space only, and ξ is a Q-Wiener process colored in space and rescaled by σ=0.05. The initial condition is a Gaussian Random Field w_0∼N(0,33/2(−Δ+49I)^{−3}) with periodic boundary conditions, and the viscosity is ν=10^{−3}. The base Navier–Stokes equation corresponds to σ=0 (no stochastic forcing). We again use the simulation code from torch-spde for these 2D datasets, on a uniform 64×64 grid. The turbulent benchmark of Section 4.2 uses the ν=10^{−5} data of Li et al. [32] on the same grid (1200 trajectories; we keep the snapshots at t≥10 and train on 9600 of them), and a 128×128 forced Navier–Stokes dataset is used only for the cost measurements of Appendix C.9. C.4 Training Configuration Description. The following tables give the hyperparameters of our runs. In all cases, the base FFM model follows the configuration in the original codebase of Kerrigan et al. [25]. We keep the neural architectures fixed and tune only the OT-related hyperparameters; for the 2D PDE datasets we additionally study the Gaussian-prior lengthscale in Appendix C.16. (1) All FFM and kFFM runs use Adam with learning rate 10^{−3} and a StepLR scheduler (step size 50, γ=0.1). (2) Table 5 gives the FNO configurations on 1D sequence datasets and Table 6 those on 1D PDE datasets. No hyperparameter search is performed for the FNO backbone; only OT-related hyperparameters are varied, so configurations are dataset-specific. “Batch Size (Sig)” is the batch size used with the signature kernel, whose memory grows with path length. (3) FNO hyperparameters on 2D PDE datasets are in Table 7. (4) Shared training settings are in Table 8. Table 5: Training hyperparameters for 1D sequence datasets (FFM with FNO backbone). Dataset | Epochs | Batch Size | Batch Size (Sig) | FNO Modes | Width | MLP Width | GP ℓ | GP σ². AEMET | 300 | 512 | 64 | 64 | 256 | 128 | 0.01 | 0.1. GeneExpr. | 300 | 512 | 128 | 16 | 256 | 128 | 0.01 | 0.1. Economy | 300 | 512 | 128 | 16 | 128 | 64 | 0.01 | 0.1. Heston | 300 | 512 | 128 | 32 | 256 | 128 | 0.01 | 0.1. Table 6: Training hyperparameters for 1D PDE datasets (FFM with FNO backbone). Dataset | Epochs | Batch Size | Batch Size (Sig) | FNO Modes | Width | MLP Width | GP ℓ | GP σ². KdV | 300 | 16 | 16 | 64 | 256 | 128 | 0.01 | 0.1. Stochastic KdV | 100 | 512 | 128 | 32 | 256 | 128 | 0.01 | 0.1. C.5 Configuration Selection Protocol. kFFM as evaluated in this paper has two per-dataset configuration choices: the OT cost entering the entropic coupling (signature kernel, Euclidean–RBF kernel, Sobolev-RBF kernel, or the raw L2 cost) and the Gaussian base measure (a smooth GP prior or white noise). The “kFFM (selected)” column

**中文:** 基础 KdV 方程仅移除 Q-Wiener 过程 ξ。KdV 数据集为一条轨迹：空间网格含 512 点，共 201 个时间步，每个时间切片作为一个样本；随机 KdV 数据集含 1200 条轨迹，每条在 128 个空间点上有 101 个时间步，同样按快照处理。方程模拟代码取自 torch-spde 代码库[43，脚注5]。Navier–Stokes 方程与随机 Navier–Stokes 方程：不可压流体的二维随机方程为 ∂_t w−νΔw=−u·∇w+f+σξ，w(0,x)=w_0(x)，(t,x)∈[0,T]×[0,1]^2；确定性外力仅依赖空间，ξ 是空间有色 Q-Wiener 过程，并按 σ=0.05 缩放。初值为高斯随机场 w_0∼N(0,33/2(−Δ+49I)^{−3})，采用周期边界，黏度 ν=10^{−3}。σ=0 时即基础 Navier–Stokes 方程（无随机强迫）。二维数据使用 torch-spde 代码，在均匀 64×64 网格上模拟。第4.2节湍流基准使用 Li 等人[32]在相同网格、ν=10^{−5} 下的数据（1200 条轨迹；保留 t≥10 的快照，并取 9600 个训练样本）；128×128 受迫 Navier–Stokes 数据仅用于附录 C.9 的计算代价测量。C.4 训练配置说明。以下各表给出实验超参数。所有基础 FFM 模型均遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]原始代码库配置。固定神经网络架构，仅调整 OT 相关超参数；二维 PDE 数据集还在附录 C.16 考察高斯先验长度尺度。(1) 所有 FFM 和 kFFM 使用 Adam，学习率 10^{−3}，StepLR 调度器（步长 50，γ=0.1）。(2) 表5给出一维序列数据集的 FNO 配置，表6给出一维 PDE 配置；不搜索 FNO 骨干网络超参数，仅调整 OT 相关项，故配置依数据集而异。“Batch Size (Sig)”是签名核批大小，其内存随路径长度增长。(3) 二维 PDE 的 FNO 超参数见表7。(4) 共享训练设置见表8。表5：一维序列数据集训练超参数（FNO 骨干 FFM）。列为数据集、训练轮数、批大小、签名核批大小、FNO 模态数、宽度、MLP 宽度、GP ℓ、GP σ²。AEMET：300、512、64、64、256、128、0.01、0.1；GeneExpr.：300、512、128、16、256、128、0.01、0.1；Economy：300、512、128、16、128、64、0.01、0.1；Heston：300、512、128、32、256、128、0.01、0.1。表6：一维 PDE 数据集训练超参数（FNO 骨干 FFM），列同表5。KdV：300、16、16、64、256、128、0.01、0.1；Stochastic KdV：100、512、128、32、256、128、0.01、0.1。C.5 配置选择流程。本文评估的 kFFM 对每个数据集有两项配置：熵耦合使用的 OT 代价（签名核、Euclidean–RBF 核、Sobolev-RBF 核或原始 L2 代价），以及高斯基准测度（平滑 GP 先验或白噪声）。“kFFM（所选）”列……（该句续至下一页）。

**Original:** Table 7: Training hyperparameters for 2D spatial PDE datasets (FFM with 2D-FNO backbone). Dataset | Epochs | Batch Size | Batch Size (Sig) | FNO Modes | Hidden Ch. | Proj. Ch. | GP ℓ | GP σ². Navier–Stokes | 300 | 512 | 512 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 0.001 | 1.0. Stoch. NS | 100 | 512 | 512 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 0.001 | 1.0. Table 8: Common training settings for all datasets. Parameter | Value: Optimizer Adam; Learning Rate 10^{−3}; LR Scheduler StepLR(step=50,γ=0.1); FFM σ_min=10^{−4}; Number of Seeds: 10 (seeds 20,…,29); 20 (seeds 20,…,2^{19}) where n=20 in Table 2; 5 on turbulent benchmark; ODE solver tolerances (atol,rtol)=10^{−5}. Table 1 is determined by a validation-based rule rather than test-set performance: for each dataset, every candidate configuration is trained identically; the configuration with the best sliced Wasserstein distance—independent of the kernels compared—on a held-out validation split is selected, then only that configuration is evaluated on the test set. In practice this rule is nearly deterministic and coincides with a simple prior-knowledge heuristic: use the signature kernel with a GP base for path-like or rough sequence data, and a bounded RBF cost (Sobolev-RBF with a GP base on the 1D KdV family, Euclidean-RBF on the Navier–Stokes family), with white-noise base selected on the Navier–Stokes family, whose fields are rougher than a smooth GP prior. On our benchmark the validated choice coincides with the best-performing configuration on all datasets except short-horizon Heston, where the top configurations are statistically indistinguishable. Pointwise diagnostics by kernel variant are reported in Appendix C.6; the cost and base measure of each selected cell are tagged in Table 1, and the Sinkhorn regularization and kernel bandwidth follow Appendix C.8 defaults, to which results are insensitive within ranges reported in the sensitivity study below. C.6 Detailed Main-Paper Results. This appendix includes a self-contained summary of the full baseline roster used in the paper, structural diagnostics, fuller pointwise comparisons, kernel ablations, and super-resolution visualizations referenced in Section 4. Additional pointwise tables follow Kerrigan et al. [25]: discretization-level mean/variance MSE summaries together with autocorrelation or log-spectrum diagnostics, and higher moments where reported. These tables focus on models for which such pointwise statistics are currently available, while Table 1 in the main text summarizes the complete baseline family comparison including CFM-OT(L2). C.7 Full Statistics Report on Sequence Datasets. For sequence datasets, in addition to mean/variance/autocorrelation tables above, we also report kurtosis and skewness in Table 14, as in Kerrigan et al. [25]. We keep them in the appendix because they complement rather than drive the main conclusion. C.8 Implementation Details and Good Practices. Algorithm 1 (Section 3.1) summarizes kFFM training; here we collect its implementation choices. Unless stated otherwise, Sinkhorn regularization is ε=0.05 on the median-normalized cost matrix, and RBF bandwidth is σ=1 on the same normalized scale (median heuristic). The coupling is computed per minibatch with these choices, which matter for stability and are recommended defaults: (i) median-normalize the per-batch cost matrix before Sinkhorn; this is the most important stability lever and makes entropic regularization ε scale-free; (ii) use log-domain Sinkhorn [38]; (iii) sample endpoint pairs from the plan rather than using barycentric projection; (iv) use a bounded (kernel) cost: results are then invariant to ε across three

**中文:** 表7：二维空间 PDE 数据集的训练超参数（采用 2D-FNO 骨干网络的 FFM）。列为数据集、训练轮数、批大小、签名核批大小、FNO 模态数、隐藏通道数、投影通道数、GP ℓ、GP σ²。Navier–Stokes：300、512、512、16、32、64、0.001、1.0；随机 NS：100、512、512、16、32、64、0.001、1.0。表8：所有数据集通用的训练设置。优化器 Adam；学习率 10^{−3}；学习率调度器 StepLR(step=50,γ=0.1)；FFM σ_min=10^{−4}；随机种子数：10（20 至 29）；表2中 n=20 时为 20（20 至 2^{19}）；湍流基准为 5；ODE 求解器容差 (atol,rtol)=10^{−5}。表1中的配置由验证集规则决定，而非测试集表现：对每个数据集，以相同方式训练所有候选配置；在留出的验证集上选择切片 Wasserstein 距离最佳的配置——该指标独立于所比较的核——随后只在测试集评估选中配置。实践中，该规则近乎确定，并与简单的先验知识启发式一致：对类似路径或粗糙序列数据，使用签名核和 GP 基准测度；对 PDE 场，使用有界 RBF 代价（KdV 一维系列采用 Sobolev-RBF 和 GP 基准测度，Navier–Stokes 系列采用 Euclidean-RBF），且 Navier–Stokes 系列选择白噪声基准测度，因为其场比平滑 GP 先验更粗糙。在本文基准中，除短时域 Heston 外，验证选择均与各数据集表现最佳的配置一致；短时域 Heston 的最佳配置之间在统计上无法区分。按核变体给出的逐点诊断见附录 C.6；表1中每个选定单元格均标注了代价和基准测度，Sinkhorn 正则化及核带宽遵循附录 C.8 的默认值，而在下述敏感性范围内结果对其不敏感。C.6 正文结果详表。本附录自包含地汇总了正文第4节引用的完整基线方法、结构诊断、更全面的逐点比较、核消融和超分辨率可视化。补充逐点表遵循 Kerrigan 等人[25]的评估约定：汇总不同离散化水平下均值/方差 MSE，并给出自相关或对数谱诊断，以及文献中报告的高阶矩。这些表聚焦于目前具备此类逐点统计数据的模型；正文表1则汇总了完整基线家族比较，包括 CFM-OT(L2)。C.7 序列数据集的完整统计报告。对于序列数据集，除上述均值/方差/自相关表外，表14还报告峰度和偏度，这也见于 Kerrigan 等人[25]。将它们放在附录中，是因为这些指标用于补充而非支撑主要结论。C.8 实现细节与良好实践。算法1（第3.1节）概述 kFFM 训练流程；本节汇总相关实现选择。除非另有说明，Sinkhorn 正则化系数 ε=0.05，作用于按中位数归一化的代价矩阵；RBF 带宽 σ=1，采用相同归一化尺度（中位数启发式）。耦合按小批量计算。以下设置对稳定性有影响，建议作为默认选项：(i) Sinkhorn 前按中位数归一化每批代价矩阵；这是最重要的稳定性措施，并使熵正则系数 ε 与尺度无关；(ii) 使用对数域 Sinkhorn[38]；(iii) 从耦合计划中采样端点对，而不是使用重心投影；(iv) 使用有界（核）代价：这样在三个……（本页末句续至下一页）。

Economy: Independent 4.90±3.26×10^{−5}, 1.08±1.13×10^{−5}, 1.72±1.02×10^{−4}; Signature 3.30±2.22×10^{−5}, 6.60±5.62×10^{−6}, 8.90±8.39×10^{−5}; RBF 3.96±2.12×10^{−5}, 6.82±3.44×10^{−6}, 1.38±0.89×10^{−4}; Euclidean 3.94±2.07×10^{−5}, 6.76±3.48×10^{−6}, 1.38±0.89×10^{−4}. Heston: Independent 6.66±4.77×10^{−3}, 1.07±0.25×10^{−1}, 2.78±2.05×10^{−4}; Signature 3.33±2.25×10^{−3}, 8.17±1.14×10^{−2}, 3.31±3.38×10^{−5}; RBF 4.48±4.18×10^{−3}, 8.27±1.53×10^{−2}, 1.41±1.03×10^{−4}; Euclidean 3.38±3.81×10^{−3}, 8.40±0.77×10^{−2}, 9.08±7.99×10^{−5}. In four orders of magnitude, Sinkhorn converges faster (15–25% faster end-to-end training than with unbounded L2 cost; Table 15); with raw L2 cost keep ε≤0.1 (we observed ≈10× degradation at ε≥0.5 on long volatility paths). (v) Per-batch recoupling was stable on all datasets. (vi) Batch size. Minibatch kernel OT induces the expected minibatch plan π̄_b (Section 3.1); in a sweep over b∈{64,128,256,512} on Heston and Stoch. KdV, kFFM tracks FFM across the 8× range, and the only seed-consistent edge favors kFFM at largest batch (Stoch. KdV, b=512: 3/3 seeds, 0.11 vs. 0.52×10^{−3} MMD-RBF). C.9 Compute and Memory Cost. Table 15 summarizes measured coupling-step cost, profiled on Heston, Stoch. KdV, and Navier–Stokes at 64² and 128² for batch sizes b∈{64,128,256,512}. End-to-end overhead is 5–7% at the largest resolution we train (128²), and because O(b²) coupling cost is independent of model size and resolution, its relative weight shrinks as the backbone grows. Bounded RBF cost also conditions Sinkhorn iterations (the Gibbs kernel e^{−c_κ/ε} stays bounded away from 0 and 1 across batches; Theorem 1), making it about 8× faster than unbounded-L2 Sinkhorn at b=512. Signature-kernel memory grows with path length, motivating the selection rule to assign RBF or L2 costs to very long sequences.

KdV，b=512：3/3 个种子，MMD-RBF 为 0.11 对 0.52×10^{−3}）。C.9 计算与内存代价。表15汇总了耦合步骤在 Heston、随机 KdV、Navier–Stokes 上测得的开销；分辨率为 64² 和 128²，批大小 b∈{64,128,256,512}。在本文训练的最大分辨率（128²）下，端到端开销为 5–7%；由于 O(b²) 的耦合代价与模型大小和分辨率无关，骨干网络增大时其相对占比会下降。有界 RBF 代价还改善了 Sinkhorn 迭代的条件数（Gibbs 核 e^{−c_κ/ε} 在各批次上保持远离 0 和 1；定理1），这使得 b=512 时其速度约为无界 L2 Sinkhorn 的 8 倍。签名核的内存需求随路径长度增长，因此选择规则会对超长序列指定 RBF 或 L2 代价。

C.11 Turbulent Navier–Stokes: Samples and Statistics. Table 17 gives the turbulent benchmark of Section 4.2 with explicit configurations and unscaled values, and Figure 4 shows vorticity samples (pooled vorticity PDFs and energy spectra in Figure 1). We also reran standard Navier–Stokes configurations of Section 4.1 with same physics diagnostics (3 seeds): flow-matching family leads (enstrophy W1 0.16 vs 0.28 for DDPM; vorticity-PDF W1 0.14–0.15 vs 0.39), and kFFM matches FFM within noise on physics metrics while retaining its distributional edge from Table 1. Kernel-cost mismatch is larger in turbulent regime: prior-data L2 distances more than double relative to standard benchmark (median 91 vs 40), pushing RBF cost deeper into saturation, where Theorem 2 is weakest; empirical results carry the method there. C.12 Measuring the Kernel-Cost Mismatch. Analytically, c_RBF(f,g)=2(1−e^{−||f−g||²/(2σ²)}) is a rescaled L2 cost up to relative error O((D/σ)²), with D the batch diameter; median normalization cancels rescaling, so Δ_κ is small at large bandwidth. Mismatch is measured on training-faithful batches via rank correlation between kernel and L2 cost matrices and total variation (TV) distance between induced plans. (1) Where RBF is not saturated it is a monotone transform of L2 (rank correlation ≈1: Gene Expression 1.000, Heston 0.98), same geometry with compressed outliers. (2) Signature kernel genuinely reranks pairs (rank correlation with L2 only 0.33–0.37 on AEMET and Gene Expression), where its wins are largest and most significant; Δ_κ is large by design, and Theorem 2 controls deviation from Wasserstein with the chosen cost rather than proximity to L2 OT. (3) On smooth Navier–Stokes fields plans agree across costs (plan TV≤0.02), the small-Δ_κ regime where the bound is tightest and bounded-cost stability and speed are operative benefits. C.13 Synthetic Couplings: Where L2 Pairing Fails. Figure 5 isolates the two failure modes of L2 pairing discussed in Section 4.1 on synthetic data under the training coupling protocol (stable over 6 seeds): a conditioning failure under heavy-tailed nuisance

DDPM 为 0.39）；在物理指标上，kFFM 与 FFM 的差异处于噪声范围，同时保留表1所示的分布优势。湍流状态下核代价失配更大：先验与数据之间的 L2 距离中位数相较标准基准增加一倍以上（91 对 40），使 RBF 代价更深地进入饱和区，而定理2在此区域最弱；该情形下方法的表现由实证结果支持。C.12 核代价失配的测量。从解析上看，c_RBF(f,g)=2(1−e^{−||f−g||²/(2σ²)}) 是经缩放的 L2 代价，其相对误差为 O((D/σ)²)，其中 D 是批次直径；中位数归一化会消去缩放，因此带宽较大时 Δ_κ 较小。作者在符合训练流程的批次上，通过核代价矩阵与 L2 代价矩阵的秩相关，以及诱导耦合计划之间的总变差（TV）距离来测量失配。(1) RBF 未饱和时，它是 L2 的单调变换（秩相关约为 1：Gene Expression 为 1.000，Heston 为 0.98），即保持相同几何结构、压缩离群值。(2) 签名核会真正改变样本对排序（AEMET 和 Gene Expression 上与 L2 的秩相关仅为 0.33–0.37），而签名核在这些数据类别上的优势最大且最显著；Δ_κ 因设计而较大，定理2控制的是所选代价下与 Wasserstein 距离的偏差，而非与 L2 OT 的接近程度。(3) 对平滑 Navier–Stokes 场，不同代价下的耦合计划一致（计划 TV≤0.02）；这是 Δ_κ 较小、理论界最紧，且有界代价的稳定性和速度发挥作用的区域。C.13 合成耦合：L2 配对失效之处。图5在训练耦合流程下的合成数据上（6 个随机种子均稳定）隔离展示了第4.1节讨论的 L2 配对两类失效模式：重尾干扰下的条件数失效……（本页末句续至下一页）。

**Original:** C.14 Why Straightness Arguments Do Not Directly Transfer

**中文:** C.14 为什么直线性论证不能直接迁移

<span id="S216"></span>
**Source:** p.35 S216

**Original:** Finite-dimensional OT-CFM is often motivated by the Euclidean W_2 picture: a better coupling, combined with linear interpolation, can lead to straighter paths and hence lower numerical integration cost. That intuition depends on geometric facts that are specific to the finite-dimensional Wasserstein setting and should not be treated as automatic in function space.

**中文:** 有限维 OT-CFM 常以欧几里得 W_2 图景作为动机：更好的耦合与线性插值结合，可能产生更直的路径，从而降低数值积分成本。这一直觉依赖于有限维 Wasserstein 空间特有的几何事实，不应被视为在函数空间中也自动成立。

<span id="S217"></span>
**Source:** p.35 S217

**Original:** Proposition 6 (Coupling-only role of kernel OT). Fix a coupling π∈Π(μ_0,μ_1). In kFFM, conditional on (f_0,f_1)∼π, the path is g_t^{f_0,f_1}=t f_1+σ_t f_0, σ_t=1−(1−σ_min)t, so that ∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0, ∂_t²g_t^{f_0,f_1}=0.

**中文:** 命题 6（核 OT 仅起耦合作用）。固定耦合 π∈Π(μ_0,μ_1)。在 kFFM 中，给定 (f_0,f_1)∼π 后，路径为 g_t^{f_0,f_1}=t f_1+σ_t f_0，其中 σ_t=1−(1−σ_min)t，因此 ∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0，且 ∂_t²g_t^{f_0,f_1}=0。
