**Original:** The conditional path is affine in t and deterministic given the pair, and its geometry does not depend on π. Moreover, the training objective can be written as L_CFM^π(θ)=E_{t,(f_0,f_1)∼π}||∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}−v_θ(g_t^{f_0,f_1},t)||_F². Therefore, optimizing over π changes only the distribution of endpoint pairs presented to the model; it does not introduce any explicit pathwise straightness, geodesic, or action-minimization term.

**中文:** 条件路径关于 t 呈仿射形式，给定端点对后是确定性的，其几何形态不依赖于 π。此外，训练目标可写为 L_CFM^π(θ)=E_{t,(f_0,f_1)∼π}||∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}−v_θ(g_t^{f_0,f_1},t)||_F²。因此，对 π 的优化只会改变输入模型的端点对分布；它不会显式引入逐路径直线性、测地线或作用量最小化项。

<span id="S219"></span>
**Source:** p.36 S219

**Original:** Proof. The path and its velocity are exactly those of Section 3.1; they coincide with the FFM path t f_1+σ_t ξ and conditional field f_1−(1−σ_min)ξ of Section 2.1, with base noise ξ replaced by the paired prior sample f_0. Differentiating the affine path gives the displayed derivatives. Substituting the conditional path into the conditional flow matching objective yields the displayed loss. Since π enters only through the sampling law of (f_0,f_1) and no additional functional of the path t↦g_t^{f_0,f_1} appears in the objective, the method does not explicitly optimize any global path-geometry quantity.

**中文:** 证明。该路径及其速度与第3.1节完全一致；它们对应于第2.1节中的 FFM 路径 t f_1+σ_t ξ 和条件向量场 f_1−(1−σ_min)ξ，只是将基准噪声 ξ 替换为配对后的先验样本 f_0。对仿射路径求导即得上文所示导数。将条件路径代入条件流匹配目标可得上文所示损失。由于 π 只通过 (f_0,f_1) 的采样分布进入目标函数，且目标中没有出现路径 t↦g_t^{f_0,f_1} 的其他泛函，因此该方法并未显式优化任何全局路径几何量。

**Original:** Corollary 2 (Coupling-invariance of ambient path straightness). For a twice differentiable path m:[0,1]→F, define the ambient acceleration and speed-variation functionals A(m):=∫_0^1||∂_t²m_t||_F²dt, V(m):=∫_0^1(||∂_tm_t||_F−∫_0^1||∂_sm_s||_Fds)²dt. Then for every coupling π∈Π(μ_0,μ_1) and every endpoint pair (f_0,f_1)∼π, the kFFM conditional path g_t^{f_0,f_1} satisfies A(g^{f_0,f_1})=0, V(g^{f_0,f_1})=0. Hence these natural ambient-space straightness measures of the prescribed conditional path are identical for all couplings.

**中文:** 推论 2（环境空间路径直线性对耦合不变）。对二次可微路径 m:[0,1]→F，定义环境加速度泛函和速度变化泛函：A(m):=∫_0^1||∂_t²m_t||_F²dt，V(m):=∫_0^1(||∂_tm_t||_F−∫_0^1||∂_sm_s||_Fds)²dt。则对任意耦合 π∈Π(μ_0,μ_1) 以及任意端点对 (f_0,f_1)∼π，kFFM 条件路径 g_t^{f_0,f_1} 满足 A(g^{f_0,f_1})=0、V(g^{f_0,f_1})=0。因此，对该指定条件路径而言，这些自然的环境空间直线性度量对所有耦合均相同。

<span id="S221"></span>
**Source:** p.37 S221

**Original:** Proof. By Proposition 6, ∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0 is constant in t and ∂_t²g_t^{f_0,f_1}=0. Therefore A(g^{f_0,f_1})=0. The speed ||∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}||_F=||f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0||_F is also constant in t, so it coincides with its own time average and V(g^{f_0,f_1})=0.

**中文:** 证明。由命题6，∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}=f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0 与 t 无关，且 ∂_t²g_t^{f_0,f_1}=0。因此 A(g^{f_0,f_1})=0。速度 ||∂_t g_t^{f_0,f_1}||_F=||f_1−(1−σ_min)f_0||_F 也与 t 无关，故等于其时间平均值，从而 V(g^{f_0,f_1})=0。

<span id="S222"></span>
**Source:** p.37 S222

**Original:** Proposition 6 and Corollary 2 formalize the internal reason that the usual OT-CFM straightness argument does not transfer directly to kFFM: kernel OT acts only at the level of endpoint coupling, while the interpolation template remains the same affine FFM path. Any improvement in convergence or sampling cost could therefore only arise indirectly through better endpoint pairings and the learned velocity field, not from an explicit path-straightness objective, and we do not claim one. Our setting is different in a second, geometric way. FFM is formulated on Gaussian measures over separable Hilbert spaces, where absolute continuity and transport already depend on Cameron–Martin and Feldman–Hájek type conditions rather than Lebesgue-density arguments. Even when one restricts…

**中文:** 命题6和推论2形式化说明了通常 OT-CFM 直线路径论证为何不能直接迁移到 kFFM：核 OT 仅作用于端点耦合层面，而插值模板仍是相同的仿射 FFM 路径。因此，收敛或采样代价的任何改进只能间接来自更好的端点配对和学得的速度场，而非显式路径直线性目标；本文不作此类主张。我们的设置还在第二个几何方面有所不同。FFM 建立在可分 Hilbert 空间上的高斯测度之上，其中绝对连续性和输运依赖 Cameron–Martin 与 Feldman–Hájek 类型条件，而非 Lebesgue 密度论证。即使将讨论限制于……（本句续至下一页）

**Original:** attention to Gaussian optimal transport, the appropriate geometry is the Bures–Wasserstein geometry rather than the flat Euclidean geometry used by standard OT-CFM heuristics.

**中文:** 若进一步聚焦于高斯最优传输，适当的几何应为 Bures–Wasserstein 几何，而不是标准 OT-CFM 启发式所用的平坦欧几里得几何。

<span id="S224"></span>
**Source:** p.38 S224

**Original:** Recent work by Yun and Zemel [52] makes this distinction explicit. For Gaussian measures on separable Hilbert spaces, they show that the optimal transport map can fail to admit the usual Brenier variational interpretation: formally, it is the subgradient of a convex function that is infinite almost everywhere, and the geodesic structure between degenerate Gaussian measures is richer than the classical finite-dimensional McCann interpolation picture. This does not mean that efficient flows are impossible in function space; rather, it means that the usual “OT implies straighter paths” argument is not a theorem in the present setting.

**中文:** Yun 和 Zemel[52]近期的工作明确揭示了这一差异。他们证明，对于可分 Hilbert 空间上的高斯测度，最优传输映射可能不具备通常的 Brenier 变分解释：形式上，它是一个几乎处处取无穷值的凸函数的次梯度；退化高斯测度之间的测地线结构，也比经典有限维 McCann 插值图景更丰富。这并不意味着函数空间中不可能存在高效流；而是说，在本文设置下，“OT 会带来更直的路径”这一常见论断并非定理。

<span id="S225"></span>
**Source:** p.38 S225

**Original:** For this reason, our paper does not claim that kFFM is geodesic or pathwise optimal in a Bures–Wasserstein sense. The theoretical guarantees in this paper concern the well-posedness, error decomposition, and discretization invariance of the kernel OT surrogate, not global straightness of the learned ODE.

**中文:** 因此，本文不声称 kFFM 在 Bures–Wasserstein 意义下具有测地线性质或逐路径最优性。本文的理论保证针对核 OT 替代目标的良定性、误差分解和离散化不变性，而非所学习 ODE 的全局直线性。

<span id="S226"></span>
**Source:** p.38 S226

**Original:** This is also why Section 4 makes no efficiency claim: the main supported claim of the paper is better distributional matching from geometry-aware coupling, and Appendix C.15 reports only the training-time convergence of the target metric.

**中文:** 这也是第4节不作效率主张的原因：本文得到支持的主要结论是几何感知耦合能够改善分布匹配；附录 C.15 仅报告目标度量在训练期间的收敛情况。

<span id="S227"></span>
**Source:** p.38 S227

**Original:** C.15 Target-Metric Convergence During Training

**中文:** C.15 训练期间目标度量的收敛

<span id="S228"></span>
**Source:** p.38 S228

**Original:** In infinite-dimensional OT-CFM, optimal transport is often motivated by improved convergence and straighter flows [48]. As discussed in Appendix C.14, we do not treat such properties as theoretical consequences in function space and make no efficiency claim. We report only the convergence of the target metric during training: Table 18 and Figure 6 show the MMD-RBF of FFM and kFFM over…

**中文:** 在无限维 OT-CFM 中，最优传输常以改善收敛和获得更直的流为动机[48]。如附录 C.14 所述，本文不将这些性质视为函数空间中的理论推论，也不作效率主张。本文只报告训练期间目标度量的收敛情况：表18和图6展示了 FFM 与 kFFM 的 MMD-RBF 随训练变化的结果……（本句续至下一页）

**Original:** Figure 5 visual region: entropic minibatch coupling plans for three synthetic scenarios and four costs.

**中文:** 图像按本轮315篇规则省略；请参阅原文 PDF 第39页。

*图像依本轮 315 篇全文翻译规则省略；保留原文图注及正文说明。*

<span id="C009"></span>
**Source:** p.39 C009

**Original:** Figure 5: Entropic minibatch couplings (b=64, ε=0.01, median-normalized costs as in training) for three synthetic scenarios (rows) and four costs (columns). Sources and targets are sorted so the intended matching is the diagonal, and each panel reports plan mass on intended matches (a uniform plan gives ≈0.14–0.19). Top: shifted bumps matched by location; every cost recovers the matching. Middle: bumps where 40% of samples carry large, independent nuisance bursts. Burst pairs dominate the median normalization of the unbounded L2 cost, collapsing clean-pair contrasts below ε, so its plan degenerates to near-uniform (0.16); bounded RBF saturates the bursts and recovers the matching (0.58), invariant to burst amplitude (the conditioning effect of Theorem 1). Bottom: Brownian paths f=vW(t), v∼U[0.5,2], matched by volatility. The expected L2 cost is separable, E||f−g||²=(v²+w²)∫t dt, so it carries no assortative signal in expectation and its plan stays near-uniform (0.25); the signature kernel (lead-lag) reads volatility through quadratic variation and concentrates on the intended matching (0.43). No single cost wins every scenario, motivating the per-data-class selection rule of Appendix C.5.

**中文:** 图5：熵正则小批量耦合（b=64、ε=0.01，代价按训练时相同方式进行中位数归一化）在三个合成场景（行）和四种代价（列）下的结果。源样本和目标样本按顺序排序，使预期匹配位于对角线上；每个面板报告耦合计划分配给预期匹配的质量（均匀计划约为 0.14–0.19）。上：按位置匹配的平移凸起；所有代价均恢复了匹配。中：40% 的样本带有幅度较大的独立干扰突发。突发样本对主导无界 L2 代价的中位数归一化，使干净样本对之间的差异被压至 ε 以下，因此其耦合计划退化为近似均匀（0.16）；有界 RBF 代价会使突发影响饱和，从而恢复匹配（0.58），且对突发幅度不敏感（即定理1中的条件数效应）。下：布朗路径 f=vW(t)，v∼U[0.5,2]，按波动率匹配。期望 L2 代价可分离，E||f−g||²=(v²+w²)∫t dt，因此在期望上不包含分类配对信号，其耦合计划接近均匀（0.25）；签名核（lead-lag）通过二次变差读取波动率，并将耦合质量集中到预期匹配上（0.43）。不存在一种代价能在所有场景中胜出，这正是附录 C.5 按数据类别选择配置的动机。

<span id="T009"></span>
**Source:** p.39 T009

**Original:** Table 18: Relative MMD-RBF improvement of kFFM over FFM at the end of the convergence curves of Figure 6 (higher is better; values above 100% occur where the unbiased estimate for kFFM is negative). Both methods share the GP prior and FNO backbone, isolating coupling as the only changed component. Columns Dataset Heston | AEMET | KdV | Economy | GeneExpr. | Stoch.KdV | Stoch.NS | Navier–Stokes; ΔMMD-RBF (%) +32% | +53% | +8% | +52% | +64% | +225% | +7% | +9%.

**中文:** 表18：图6收敛曲线末端 kFFM 相对 FFM 的 MMD-RBF 改善比例（越高越好；当 kFFM 的无偏估计为负时，数值可能超过 100%）。两种方法采用相同的 GP 先验和 FNO 骨干网络，因此仅耦合方式不同。数据集：Heston｜AEMET｜KdV｜Economy｜GeneExpr.｜Stoch.KdV｜Stoch.NS｜Navier–Stokes；ΔMMD-RBF（%）：+32%｜+53%｜+8%｜+52%｜+64%｜+225%｜+7%｜+9%。

<span id="S229"></span>
**Source:** p.39 S229

**Original:** The final phase of training, with both models sharing the GP prior and FNO backbone so that the only change is the coupling. The curves are the best-so-far value (cumulative minimum) of the across-seed mean; on KdV, where the unbiased estimate is noisy at the level of single checkpoints, the curves are instead the running mean over the final phase. kFFM ends training at a better MMD-RBF on every dataset, with the largest gains on Stoch.KdV, GeneExpr., AEMET, Economy, and Heston.

**中文:** 训练最后阶段中，两种模型共享 GP 先验和 FNO 骨干网络，因此唯一变化因素是耦合方式。曲线表示跨种子均值的历史最优值（累积最小值）；对于 KdV，单个检查点上的无偏估计噪声较大，因此曲线改为训练最后阶段的运行均值。训练结束时，kFFM 在所有数据集上的 MMD-RBF 均更优，最大增益出现在 Stoch.KdV、GeneExpr.、AEMET、Economy 和 Heston。

**Original:** Figure 7: Aggregate MMD-RBF sensitivity of kFFM hyperparameters across datasets; the spread is (max−min)/|mean| over the tested settings of the hyperparameter in parentheses, and lower means less sensitivity. Signature-kernel settings are nearly invariant on the tested sequence datasets; Sinkhorn regularization is typically less influential than RBF bandwidth; and results depend more on ε under raw L2 (Euclidean) cost than under bounded RBF cost (median spread about 8% vs. near zero).

**中文:** 图7：kFFM 超参数在各数据集上的总体 MMD-RBF 敏感性。spread 定义为该超参数（括号内所示）各测试设置下的 (max−min)/|mean|，数值越低表示敏感性越弱。签名核设置在所测序列数据集上近乎不变；Sinkhorn 正则化通常比 RBF 带宽影响更小；与有界 RBF 代价相比，原始 L2（Euclidean）代价下结果对 ε 更敏感（中位 spread 约为 8% 对近零）。

<span id="S237"></span>
**Source:** p.41 S237

**Original:** Figure 7 summarizes the aggregate MMD-RBF spread across datasets for the main tunable kernel families, where spread for a given dataset is computed as (max−min)/|mean| over the tested settings. The dominant pattern is that Sinkhorn regularization is typically quite stable, while RBF bandwidth can matter more on selected datasets. Figure 8 then shows direct sweeps on representative datasets, run with the white-noise base measure: the top panel varies RBF bandwidth, and the bottom panel varies Sinkhorn regularization. We focus these direct sweeps on axes that showed the most variation; signature-kernel settings were substantially flatter on the tested sequence datasets and are summarized in Figure 7. Re-aggregating the full grids: results are indistinguishable within seed-to-seed variation for RBF bandwidths σ∈[0.1,2] on every dataset (the wider sweeps in Figure 8 show where larger bandwidths start to matter on selected datasets); Sinkhorn regularization is flat across three orders of magnitude (ε∈[0.001,1]) for bounded costs, whereas with raw L2 cost ε≥0.5 degrades long volatility paths by ≈10×; and signature hyperparameters (dyadic order 0–3, static bandwidth 0.1–5) leave results unchanged. The kernel family and base measure are the choices that matter: Euclidean costs, raw or bounded, still lose to the signature kernel by 2.6–2.9× on Gene Expression and Economy (Section 4.1).

**中文:** 图7汇总了主要可调核族在各数据集上的 MMD-RBF 总体 spread；对给定数据集，spread 是在所测超参数设置上计算的 (max−min)/|mean|。总体规律是 Sinkhorn 正则化通常相当稳定，而 RBF 带宽在某些数据集上影响更大。图8进一步展示了采用白噪声基准测度的代表性数据集直接扫描：上图改变 RBF 带宽，下图改变 Sinkhorn 正则化。直接扫描聚焦于变化最大的参数轴；签名核参数在所测序列数据集上平坦得多，因此在图7中总结。对完整网格重新汇总后发现：所有数据集上 σ∈[0.1,2] 的 RBF 带宽结果在种子间变异范围内无法区分（图8中更宽的扫描显示，较大带宽开始影响部分数据集）；有界代价下，Sinkhorn 正则化在三个数量级范围 ε∈[0.001,1] 内基本平坦；而原始 L2 代价下 ε≥0.5 会使长波动率路径上的结果恶化约 10 倍；签名核超参数（二进制分割阶数 0–3、静态带宽 0.1–5）不改变结果。真正起作用的是核族和基准测度：在 Gene Expression 和 Economy 上，无论原始还是有界 Euclidean 代价，其表现仍比签名核差 2.6–2.9 倍（第4.1节）。

<span id="S238"></span>
**Source:** p.41 S238

**Original:** The choice of Gaussian prior matters more on the 2D PDE datasets (Navier–Stokes and Stoch.NS), independently of the kernel. Figure 9 shows sensitivity of these two datasets to the lengthscale of the Gaussian prior, which encodes a prior belief about data smoothness.

**中文:** 高斯先验的选择对二维 PDE 数据集（Navier–Stokes 和 Stoch.NS）的影响更大，且与核的选择无关。图9展示这两个数据集对高斯先验长度尺度的敏感性；该长度尺度编码了关于数据平滑性的先验判断。

<span id="S239"></span>
**Source:** p.41 S239

**Original:** D Extended Related Work

**中文:** D 扩展相关工作

<span id="S240"></span>
**Source:** p.41 S240

**Original:** This section expands the discussion of Section 5. The study of using optimal transport [49] to improve flow matching was first proposed by Pooladian et al. [39] and Tong et al. [48], who add an OT-induced coupling of each minibatch before interpolation and training. Several follow-up works [28,51] have aimed to further expand the framework. All have assumed that the sample space is R^d. To better capture the resolution-invariance of grid data and continuity of path data, Kerrigan et al. [25] first proposed Functional Flow Matching, a framework to perform flow matching in Hilbert space relying on Gaussian measure theory. However, OT-based endpoint coupling had not been brought to this space.

**中文:** 本节扩展第5节的讨论。利用最优传输[49]改进流匹配的研究最早由 Pooladian 等人[39]和 Tong 等人[48]提出：他们在插值和训练之前，为每个小批量加入由 OT 诱导的耦合。后续有若干工作[28,51]尝试进一步扩展该框架，但均假设样本空间为 R^d。为更好地捕获网格数据的分辨率不变性和路径数据的连续性，Kerrigan 等人[25]首先提出 Functional Flow Matching，这是一个依赖高斯测度理论、在 Hilbert 空间中执行流匹配的框架。不过，基于 OT 的端点耦合此前尚未引入该空间。

<span id="S241"></span>
**Source:** p.41 S241

**Original:** The line of research that embeds probability measures in RKHS was proposed by the pioneering work of Gretton et al. [20] and Sriperumbudur et al. [46], where kernel-induced metrics, such as Integral Probability Metrics (IPM), have been studied extensively [10,46] and used for two-sample hypothesis testing [20], most recently in path space [6] with the signature kernel. The IPM in the form of the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) has been linked with both probability divergences and…

**中文:** 将概率测度嵌入再生核 Hilbert 空间（RKHS）的研究由 Gretton 等人[20]和 Sriperumbudur 等人[46]的开创性工作提出。此后，核诱导度量（例如积分概率度量，IPM）得到广泛研究[10,46]，并用于双样本假设检验[20]；近期还结合签名核扩展到了路径空间[6]。以最大均值差异（MMD）形式表示的 IPM 已与概率散度以及……建立联系（本句续至下一页）。

**Original:** Wasserstein distances [16], and it has an entropic OT formulation in the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence of Li et al. [31]. It has also been used as the objective of gradient flows [1,18], which have been adopted in deep generative models. The extension of these frameworks to function space is an active area of research, and to our knowledge our work is the first to explore it in the context of flow matching.

**中文:** Wasserstein 距离[16]；此外，Li 等人[31]提出的 Hilbert Sinkhorn 散度包含其熵正则 OT 形式。该度量也被用作梯度流的目标函数[1,18]，并已应用于深度生成模型。将这些框架扩展至函数空间仍是活跃的研究方向；据作者所知，本文首次在流匹配背景下探索这一问题。

<span id="S243"></span>
**Source:** p.43 S243

**Original:** Concurrent function-space flow matching. Three concurrent works are closest to ours; none studies the endpoint coupling. Kollovieh et al. [27] use flow matching with GP priors for probabilistic time-series forecasting, a conditional task with independent prior-data coupling; this is closest in spirit to our GP-prior setup and orthogonal on the axis we study, and our results suggest that framework could itself benefit from kernel-OT coupling. Zhang and Scott [53] straighten flows in Hilbert space via rectification, iteratively re-training on model-generated endpoint pairs; rectification changes pairs across training rounds using the model, whereas kernel OT changes pairs within each batch using data geometry in a single run and extends beyond Hilbert spaces (signature kernel on path space), so the two can in principle be composed. Lee and Flouris [30] propose operator flow matching for time-series forecasting, again conditional prediction with independent coupling. On the finite-dimensional side, Fatras et al. [13,14] analyze minibatch OT plans and the expected-minibatch coupling that Section 3.1 relies on.

**中文:** 并行开展的函数空间流匹配研究。与本文最接近的有三项同期工作；它们均未研究端点耦合。Kollovieh 等人[27]使用带 GP 先验的流匹配进行概率时间序列预测，这是一种先验与数据独立耦合的条件预测任务；其 GP 先验设置与本文思路最接近，但研究维度与本文不同。本文结果表明，该框架本身也可能受益于核 OT 耦合。Zhang 和 Scott[53]通过 rectification 在 Hilbert 空间中拉直流，并迭代地使用模型生成的端点对重新训练；rectification 借助模型在不同训练轮次间改变配对，而核 OT 在单次运行中依据数据几何在每个批次内改变配对，并且可扩展到 Hilbert 空间之外（例如路径空间上的签名核），因此原则上两者可以组合。Lee 和 Flouris[30]提出用于时间序列预测的算子流匹配，同样属于采用独立耦合的条件预测。有限维方面，Fatras 等人[13,14]分析了小批量 OT 计划及第3.1节所依赖的期望小批量耦合。


## 阅读提示

- kFFM 的核心改动是以核诱导熵正则 OT 替换 FFM 的独立端点配对，同时保持神经算子架构不变（摘要 S003）。
- 论文报告多个时间序列与 PDE 基准上的分布匹配改进，但未将路径直线性或效率提升作为理论结论（C.14，S225–S226）。
- 理论结果聚焦于核 OT 替代目标的良定性、误差分解与离散化不变性；结果解释应结合表格中的基准测度、核配置和指标定义。
